It holds the lines #98's fixture plays (§ 5.2 Ch1 intro) and the § 5.3 recurring lines with speakers. #102 copies in the rest. Lint rule 10 reads it (unchanged, plus a test).
8. **0-2 isn't registered yet** (#106), so the change-of-mind fixture is `lab-change-of-mind` on the `lab-lamp` stage (a night light, then the bead revealed by the mobile), like the other lab stages. #106 adds `c0p2-change-of-mind` on the real cushion.

**Why.** The bubble is the main teaching device, so a player must see every change of mind. Dialogue must never hide the baby, the goal or the touchables, and every word must pass the grandparent rule at the smallest phone.

**Consequences.**
- #102 fills `SCRIPT` and plays the chapter intros through the queue. #99's word speech bubbles can reuse `barkCard` and `layoutTalk`. #106 points the change-of-mind fixture at 0-2.
- New visual fixtures: `bubble-reaching`, `lab-change-of-mind`, `subtitle-3`. Fixtures now pass their last tick's events to the page (`PagePlayer` / `PageSession` `events`), so barks show in stills, and `show.talk` puts lines on the page.

### D-62 · 2026-10-19 · Words on the page: the ring, the learn beat, the translations and the tutorial (#99)

**Context.** #99 builds the word ring (GDD § 2.4; popup art bible § 7), the big speech bubble and effect of a said word, the learn beat and word card (§ 5.4), the one-time Mocha translation, the 嗶！ first-use tutorial (1-3 notes) and the 「要！」 answer to a tap before any word (§ 6 risk 1). The GDD says the ring opens "around him", the bible says the discs "fan out from the portrait like a volvelle", and the issue asks for one-handed reach and 左手模式; the order of the learn beat, the card's timing and what a "one-time translation per word per page" is on the learn page itself were open.

**Decision.**
1. **Where the ring is.** Always around Baokaka (a tap on him or on the portrait opens the same ring): a kraft volvelle wheel with a printed scale and a dotted gold track, the words as 72 px cream discs on it (≥ 64 px; 20 px glyphs), 「好了」 straight under him. The words start beside 「好了」 on the thumb's side (lower right; lower left in 左手模式, mirrored) and go round over the top in learning order, ≤ 44° apart, so one or two words sit in the lower half within one thumb's reach; eight words and 「好了」 close the circle. If the ring would leave the safe area (16 px gutters, below the HUD, above the bar) its centre slides in; on a 320 px phone a full ring closes its gaps (`view/words/ringLayout.ts`). It opens like a volvelle turning (each disc swings round from 「好了」, 45 ms apart; fades under reduced motion). The page dims 30 % except a pool of light on him. A tap anywhere off a disc, 「好了」 or Escape closes it with no effect.
2. **Seal colours and icons.** Each word has its own deep seal colour (new palette tokens `wordBeep` … `wordTogether`, `wordGrowth`; none marigold) and a cream line icon on a torn-edge seal stamp (`view/words/icons.ts`). The new word's disc wears the marigold halo and dashed ring (it is the touchable to try) until first said.
3. **What the ring offers** is exactly what `core/step` accepts as a `say`: `wordAvailable` over the § 2.4 order (`game/words.ts` `ringWords`). So a replayed page offers its designed words (later words hidden, #93), and a page's own `learns` word joins once learned. With none, a tap on him shows 「要！」 as his bark with a dotted chalk pointer to his target (`view/words/poke.ts`) and no ring.
4. **Saying.** A disc queues `{ t: 'say', word }` and closes the ring. On the say tick the word is his bark at 1.6× (the big speech bubble, placed by `layoutTalk`), and the word's reach widens from him in its seal colour (`SayFx.tsx`: 嗶！ 120, 掰掰 / 哇！ 160, 咻—— its 90° cone, 飛機！ the page, a small burst for 不要！ / 抱抱 / 一起). `PagePlayer` `onSay(word, motif)` is the audio hook; `WORD_MOTIF` gives each word a distinct note pattern for #105.
5. **The learn beat** starts on the `learn` event: source → Baokaka → Mocha go through the subtitle queue as one beat (tap to advance, like every line; sources that make no sound, growth grunts and 第一步's own lines are left out, § 5.4). When those lines are gone the **word card** drops in (a taped index card: seal, the word big, its effect: 「嗶！：閘門開開」); the world waits under it (`card` pause reason) and a tap puts it away. The ring can open while the lines are still up, not while the card is. Lines live in `content/script.ts` (`learn.<key>.src|baby|mocha|then`), the beats and cards in `content/words.ts` for all 11 § 5.4 entries.
6. **One-time translation per word per page.** The first say of a word on a page queues Mocha's translation — except on its learn page, where the beat just played it: there it is the follow-up if the word has one (嗶！: 「……閘門真的開了。」), else nothing. Counted per page session (重來 starts over).
7. **The first-use tutorial** runs on the page whose `learns` word is the player's first (no other word available: 1-3). After the card, until the word is first said: the portrait glows; once he is stuck (reaching, looking, crying) the ghost hand (hint L2 on `baby:baby`, with its halo) taps him; with the ring open the hand taps the word's disc (upside down, so its glyph stays readable). "New-word glow until first use" is per page session, not saved.
8. **1-3 is not registered yet** (#107), so its flow is played on the lab stage `lab-gates` (the engine-test layout of 1-3, placeholder art) and `lab-words` (every word available). Fixtures: `word-poke`, `word-card-beep`, `word-ring-open`, `word-ring-full`, `word-say-beep`. `npm run check:play` plays `lab-gates` end to end through real pointer input (the poke, Mocha's push, the learn beat, the card, the tutorial, the ring, 嗶！, the bead), checking the world holds under the card and the ring.

**Why.** One tap to open and one to say, every word big and named, in the thumb's reach; the learn beat makes each word a memorable moment (§ 6 risk 10) and the tutorial makes sure the first one is found.

**Consequences.**
- #107 builds the real 1-3 and moves the `word-*` fixtures and the play scenario onto `c1p3`.
- #105 plays `WORD_MOTIF` from `onSay`; #120 / #128 give 抱抱 and 一起 their effects (the ring already offers them where a page allows).
- #104's 左手模式 setting already mirrors the ring through `PageChrome.leftHand`.

### D-63 · 2026-10-19 · Hints: the ladder, the worker and the fallback (#100)

**Context.** #100 builds GDD § 2.7's hint ladder on the solver (§ 3.7, D-46) with #140's halo and ghost hand. The GDD leaves open what "the stored `solution` step that matches the current part states" means when several steps share part states, when the ladder resets, how long each level shows, what Mocha does where, which § 5.3 line goes with which action, where the hint line is shown, and what "offer 重來 + step 1" looks like.

**Decision.**
1. **The source** (`core/hint.ts`, pure): `nextHint(def, state, { cap, timeMs, clock })` runs `solve(def, state, { cap: 200_000, timeMs: 300, clock, stopAtGoal: true })` and takes `nextAction(state)` only from a search that reached the goal (no second, uncapped solve). Anything else (cap, time, a cascade error, a throw) falls back silently.
2. **The fallback** (`solutionAction`): the page's `solution` is replayed once; each gesture keeps the state it was played from. Pick the first gesture whose part states equal the current ones and that still does something now (a legal candidate whose result differs from a wait). If the solution let the page settle before that gesture (`wait: 'settle'`) and the page is still moving, wait first. Parts are compared exactly, then only on the parts the solution drives (a spun 床鈴 doesn't hide the way). A match with nothing left to do, or the part states after the last gesture, mean "wait" — but only while a wait still changes something. Otherwise the answer is `restart`: 重來, then the solution's first gesture.
3. **The worker** (`workers/hint.worker.ts`) is thin: `{ id, pageId, state, def? } → { id, action | null, source }` (or `{ id, failed }`), the page by id from the registry, or as data for a page the registry doesn't hold (PageLab stages). `HintClient` never fails: no `Worker`, a worker that can't start or errors (then never retried on that page), an uncloneable message, `failed`, or no reply in 2 s → `fallbackHint` on the main thread (no search). PageLab keeps its own solver worker (D-52).
4. **The ladder** (`view/affordance/ladder.ts`, `useHintLadder.ts`): each press of 提示 asks for the next action from the state *now* and climbs 1 → 2 → 3, then stays at 3 ("show me" repeats; pressing it again and again finishes any page). The player's own move (a tap on a part, a perch or Mocha, a drag landing on a stop, a word said) resets it to 0; so do 重來 and the page done. L1 shows for 4 s (the halo pulses 3 × 1 s); L2's hand stays until the player moves or presses again; L3 shows the hand 0.9 s (0.3 s reduced), then queues the action as a real input (closing the word ring for a word), and clears 1.1 s later. Nothing is recorded; stickers never look at hints (R8).
5. **The nudge (L0)** comes after the 自動提示 delay (30 s by default; 15 s in 長輩模式 unless it is off; `autoHintMs`) with no change to the solver's key (the clock and the reach beat don't count), while nothing holds the page (a menu, the ring, a word card) and the ladder is at 0; it repeats after each further delay of stillness. It never offers 重來 by itself (a lost page is the player's call).
6. **What shows.** L0: the target's halo fades in and out once (`aff-halo-once`; static under reduced motion) and Mocha, at rest, stretches with a yawn (the `mocha.stretch` pose, now preloaded from chapter 1) turned toward it. L1: #140's halo, Mocha turned toward the target, and his line. L2/L3: the ghost hand too; for a word, the hand taps Baokaka and, with the ring open, the word's disc. A wait: no halo or hand; Mocha sits up and taps his paw (a slow bob, none under reduced motion) with 「等等看。」 for 3.2 s. A `restart` answer glows 重來 in the bottom bar (the portrait's breathing halo) and shows nothing on the page; L3 restarts and plays step 1. Mocha's cues show only while he is at rest; anything he is doing wins.
7. **Lines** (`content/hints.ts`, § 5.3 only): a tab or slider 「拉拉看這裡」, a wheel 「轉轉看」, a flap 「翻開看看」, Mocha to a perch 「讓摩卡上去」 (a box perch 「藏起來看看？」), a word 「試試看說話」, a wait 「等等看。」. A tap on a prop or light and petting Mocha have no line in § 5.3, so none (the halo and hand carry them). The line is Mocha's bark (`bark: 'hint'`) from level 1, and only while he is on the page.
8. **Fixtures** pin a level (`show.hint`, computed in-process): `hint-nudge`, `hint-level1`, `hint-ghost-hand`, `hint-wait`. `check:play` plays 1-2 with 提示 alone (`hints-c1p2`).

**Why.** The solver's answer is exact and the fallback keeps the hint useful when a phone is slow; a ladder that only climbs on a press and only resets on the player's own move never nags (R8), and "show me" that keeps going means no one is ever stuck on a page (R2, R7).

**Consequences.**
- #106 uses `GhostHand` (and `HintShow`) for the scripted 0-1 and 0-3 tutorials; the ladder runs on every page as is.
- #104's settings screen changes `autoHint` and `elder`; `GameShell` already reads them.
- #105 can hook a sound on `PageChrome.onHint` (it now only listens; the page runs the ladder).
- New pages need nothing: `pages/hints.test.ts` presses "show me" to `done` on every registered page, from the start and one move away, with the solver and with the fallback alone.

### D-64 · 2026-10-19 · Page completion, the sticker card, 淘氣 and the 土地公 (#101)

**Context.** #101 builds GDD § 2.8: the completion sequence, the three page stickers, the 淘氣 predicate, the 土地公 tap and the § 2.8 rewards. The GDD and the bible say the sticker card comes *after* the fold; #97's shell note said to put it between the grab hold and the turn. The GDD leaves open what "1.5 s" counts from, how often the pops play, where the rewards live, where page select shows the mischief, and what the turn does under reduced motion.

**Decision.**
1. **The sequence.** The grab tick barks 「這個！」 (`barksOf`: a `grab` wins over the engine's bark) and flashes a foil sparkle burst where the shiny lay (`PageView` `GrabBurst`, 650 ms, a fade only under reduced motion). The page is held `GRAB_HOLD_MS` (900 ms), the pop-ups fold flat, then the turn's **interlude** shows the sticker card; after it the leaf turns and the next page rises. `playTurn` takes the interlude and holds every running animation and pending step at `swapAt` (fold done, leaf not lifted) until it resolves. The chapter card now starts dropping just after the leaf lifts (`CARD_AFTER_LEAF_MS`), so it is never caught mid-air. Under reduced motion the interlude is at 0: the card shows over the unfolded page, then the cross-fade. After the last registered page the card shows, then the 目錄 opens.
2. **The card** (`view/hud/StickerCard.tsx`): a kraft card taped over the folded page, the page title on a cream label, the three stickers on dimples (an unearned one shows its icon's silhouette pressed into the kraft), names on cream tags, 「下一頁！」 (§ 5.3). Earned stickers slap on 200 ms apart with a squash. It stays 1.5 s **after its last sticker lands** (≈ 2.4 s in all; 1.5 s under reduced motion), or until a tap: a tap while the card is up ends the card (the turn goes on); a tap during the rest of the turn skips the turn, as before.
3. **淘氣.** `mischiefFired(def, prev, state, events)`: the page's predicate holds after the tick (the tick's `counters`, with `count`) and did not before. It is matched every tick of every attempt, finished or not, replays included. It is awarded once per attempt and saved at once (`store.earnSticker`).
4. **土地公.** A tap on him (#90's gesture event, ≥ 48 px) enqueues 「呵呵，囡仔，慢慢仔來。」 in his subtitle bubble (once in the queue however often he is tapped), earns the sticker once per attempt and saves it. On chapters 4, 7 and 10 the first tap of an attempt also queues Mocha's squint line when he is on the page. The pre-existing "drawn behind the board" bug on 1-2 (#90's note) was already fixed by #96 moving him to the board's corner (236, 98), where he peeks out once it flips; nothing more was needed.
5. **The pops.** Earning 淘氣 or 土地公 pops a kraft tag over the bar's tray: the sticker on foil and 「嘿嘿，淘氣！」 / 「找到土地公！」 (§ 5.3) on a cream label, ≈ 2 s. The tray's disc slaps on at the same moment. It plays on every attempt that earns it, also when the save already had it: the feedback answers the player's action. The save never loses a sticker (`progress.ts` only sets them); hints don't touch them.
6. **Moves.** `PageSession.moves` counts applied non-wait inputs (hint "show me" plays count too) since the page started or 重來; the shell passes `{ mischief, tudigong, moves }` to `completePage` (`bestPar`).
7. **Rewards are derived, not stored.** `rewardFlags(save)` counts 土地公 and 淘氣 over registered pages; `bonusPage` / `mischiefCover` need one on each of the game's 65 pages (`GAME_PAGES`, § 4.2 + page E), so a half-built registry never unlocks them. No save schema change.
8. **Page select.** The 目錄 lists, under the page strip, each **solved** page's 淘氣: its 嘿 sticker (foil when earned, a pressed silhouette when not), its number and its `mischief.text`. Nothing for a page not yet solved (no clutter on a first play). `UI.contents.mischief` (「淘氣」, the list's accessible name) and `UI.card.next` are new strings.

**Why.** The GDD's order (fold → card → next page) keeps the card on a quiet, flat page, and holding the turn rather than splitting it keeps #97's single timeline. Counting the 1.5 s after the stickers land gives the slaps their moment. Deriving rewards keeps the save tiny and can't drift from the stickers.

**Consequences.**
- #103's album reads `rewardFlags(save).mischiefCover` for the 「淘氣大王」 cover; the bonus page (M4) reads `bonusPage`.
- #105 can sound the slap, the pop and 「這個！」 (they are `data-` marked: `[data-card-sticker]`, `[data-sticker-pop]`, `[data-grab-burst]`).
- Fixtures: `c1p2-grab`, `sticker-card`, `sticker-mischief`, `sticker-tudigong`, `contents-mischief`; `check:play` runs `stickers-c1p2` (all three of 1-2's stickers in the shell, the card, the 目錄).

### D-65 · 2026-10-19 · The chapter card, its lines and the script for chapters 0–1 (#102)

**Context.** #102 builds the departure-board chapter card (GDD § 2.13, § 5.2; bible § 7, § 2.4) and copies every prologue and chapter 1 line into `content/script.ts`. #97's chapter turn hung a simple card for ≈ 2.4 s. Several points are left open:
- where the narration lines play ("over the card or the first page") and how the turn waits for them;
- when the card shows outside a turn (the GDD flow has 目錄 → chapter card → page);
- the prologue's 下一站;
- how the bible's 「第N章 · title」 fits a 296 px card;
- how speech is stored.

**Decision.**
1. **The card's lines hold the turn.** `playTurn` gains a second hold, `TurnHooks.card`, at the card's `settle` (after the drop and two swings, `CARD_MS.drop` 820 ms). While it holds, only the camera, the card and what has not started yet are paused. What is already moving keeps going: the leaf, the confetti, and the new page rising under the card in a chapter turn. The lines play under the card in beats of ≤ 3, a tap a beat, through the shell's talk queue (`game/talk.ts`), with no timer (§ 2.13). After the last tap the card lifts (300 ms) and the camera comes back in. A tap before the lines appear skips the whole turn, as #97 had it. While the lines are up, the card layer is a modal (`aria-modal`; the book is `aria-hidden`), and its own full-screen button (「下一句」) takes the taps.
2. **Which lines.** `cardLines(chapter)` in `content/chapters.ts`:
   - The prologue's card plays P lines 1–5: the eve of the birthday, 阿嬤's two lines, Mocha falls asleep.
   - Page 0-1 opens on lines 6–12: midnight, the little cloud, the treasures and the book blown away, Baokaka's 「……哇。」. This puts the "night of the incident" on the page it happens on.
   - Chapter 1's card plays all four of its lines.
   - Chapters 2–10 add theirs with their content issues.
3. **The chapter open.** Opening a chapter's first page from the cover or the 目錄 (「開始」 and 「繼續」 included) plays the card first, as a new turn kind, `'open'` (≈ 1.9 s). The book already lies on the quilt in the 3/4 view, with its pop-ups flat. The card drops, its lines play, it lifts, the camera comes in, and the pop-ups rise. 重來 and a turn into the page don't open it. The test is `opensChapter`: the first *registered* page of its chapter, so 1-2 stands in for 1-1 until #107. Under reduced motion every card fades in, holds and fades out.
4. **A page's intro waits.** A page held at its start (a turn bringing it in, an `'open'`) keeps its `lines.intro` until the hold ends. The shell holds an opening page from its first frame, so its intro never shows under the card.
5. **Layout.** The chapter is set vertically on the red spine (「第一章」, like a book's spine). The title is in red serif on the first row, beside the time chip. This keeps 「第十章 · 離天空最近的地方」 inside the card. The spine and the 翻 seal use the deep seal red (`sealDark`): `seal` red fails 7:1 under cream lettering. The landmark is 36 px, smaller only for a six-character name (大安森林公園). The board has one split-flap tile per character, digits included (台北101 is five tiles).
6. **下一站.** It is `ChapterInfo.next`, copied from § 5.2 (公館, 西門, 劍潭, 動物園, 新北投 …; ≤ 8 characters). The prologue's is 家: the 月亮線 starts from home, and the GDD names none.
7. **The script.** Keys follow the § 5.2 headings: `p.intro.N`, `c0p3.join.N`, `c0p4.train.N`, `c1.intro.N`, `c1p1.pack.N`, `c1p5.clerk.N`, `c1p6.end.N`. Speech is stored without its outer 「」 (the bubble is the quote), as #98's lines were; inner 『』 stay. 咻咻's prologue lines carry the label 「小小的雲」. The § 5.7 UI strings that have no screen yet (photo, settings, bonus) are in `UI` for #103, #104 and #135. New strings not in § 5.7: 「跳過」 and 「下一句」, the tap layers' accessible names.
8. **QA.** Text and touchables under an open modal are no longer measured by `check:ui`: they are out of reach. This matches what it already did for text under the pause menu. They still count as the page's touchables.

**Why.** Holding the existing timeline keeps one turn, one tap layer and one skip rule. Letting what is already moving finish avoids pop-ups freezing mid-rise under the card. Splitting the prologue lets the card set the scene and the page tell the incident, without a 12-line card. Opening a chapter with its card from the cover makes 「開始」 begin with the prologue's story, as the GDD's flow intends.

**Consequences.**
- #106 wires 0-3's join lines (`lines.intro`) and 0-4's train lines (`lines.onEvent` on `partEnter train on`). #107 wires 1-1, 1-5 and 1-6. With 1-1 registered, chapter 1's card opens before 1-1.
- #105 can sound the drop, the flaps and the lift: `[data-chapter-board]`, `[data-flap]`.
- #110's playtest: the prologue card plus the 0-1 intro is five taps of reading before play. Watch whether children wait for it.
- Fixtures: `chapter-card-p` and `chapter-card-c1`. `check:play` runs `prologue-card`, from a fresh save to 0-1's intro.

### D-66 · 2026-10-19 · The chapter spread, the photo moment and the album page (#103)

**Context.** #103 builds GDD § 2.9: the spread, the photo moment and the album page with its four notes (§ 5.6). Page 1-6 itself is #107's, and the photo frames are still to be drawn by the character artist. Several points are left open:
- what "2 edges from the treasure with a clear path" means in the engine;
- what the slow-motion approach is, since the simulation is paused;
- which Mocha line Frame A takes when Mocha is not in the photo;
- how the pool formula numbers the frames;
- when a photo is saved, and how a retake's choice works;
- where the album sits in the flow before #113.

**Decision.**
1. **The trigger** (`game/photo.ts` `photoReady`). On a page with `spread`, the moment starts on the first tick where Baokaka's target (`selectTarget`, at the step's growth) is the goal, it is reachable (`approach` not null), and its path is 1 or 2 edges. If he reaches the treasure without passing that point (it came into reach under him), the moment plays after the grab. A spread's `onDone` waits for its photo. The session is paused with the reason `photo`; the hint ladder is held.
2. **The slow motion is a viewfinder.** The paper camera's viewfinder shows the photo as it would be taken at this instant, composed from the baked frames (`PhotoScene`). B is `reach`, crawling in toward the treasure on the floor. A is `bounce`, arms up, the treasure over his head. C is `eat`, the treasure at his mouth. +M is `mocha.album` in the bottom-left corner. The 3 s loop puts the grab (the peak) at 1.8 s: B before 1.4 s, A from 1.4 to 2.2 s, C after. The viewfinder creeps in (scale 1 → 1.09, a CSS loop) and the 「哇！」 meter rises to its star at the peak. What you see is what you get. Under reduced motion there is no zoom, and the meter stands at its peak mark; the picture still changes.
3. **Placeholder composites.** These poses stand in for the photo frames the character artist will draw (§ 2.9 requirement 2). The treasure is drawn in `view/photo/treasureArt.ts`: 雞腿 for chapter 1, a foil token with the first character for the other chapters until their art issues. A spread's goal is drawn as its treasure on the page too.
4. **Notes.** Frame A takes the chapter's § 5.6 row for all four speakers, Mocha's line included, whether he is in the photo or not ("chapter-specific Mocha lines replace these for Frame A"). B and C take each speaker's pool at `(chapter × 7 + frame) mod poolSize` with A = 0, B = 1, C = 2. Mocha's pool is `mocha.in` with +M, otherwise `mocha.out`. Chapter 1: B gives pool index 2 (Mocha 0), C gives 0 (Mocha 1). The tables are `content/album.ts`, with the ending's row for #135.
5. **Saving.** Every shot is kept the moment it is taken (`store.keepPhoto`, a save trigger, § 2.10), so "the last photo taken is kept". After a retake, the previous and the new shot lie side by side under the print, and tapping one keeps it. 「放進相簿」 goes on: the sticker card, then the chapter turn.
6. **The flow.** Spread done → the grab's 0.9 s hold → the album page over the book (the print flies in, the notes pop on 0.6 s apart) → 「再拍一次」 / 「放進相簿」. The chapter-end lines (`c1p6.end.*`) are #107's page lines. The cover's 相簿 opens the album at the latest chapter with a photo (`albumHome`), or chapter 1's empty page, with 「返回」 and, when there is a photo, 「再拍一次」. Browsing chapters is #113.
7. **The album page.** It is a cream leaf on 花磚 endpapers with the linen cover down the binding. It holds the album's name on washi, 第N章 + title + time, the instant print (the treasure's name under it), 學會了 + the word big in red serif on its seal, the 6 × 3 sticker grid (a column per page, from the registered page with that index), its count, and the four sticky notes (pink, mint, sky, lilac; never marigold). There is no handwriting font in the build (no new font payload): the notes use the rounded Chiron GoRound, tilted. The leaf scrolls on short phones; the buttons stay fixed under it.
8. **The spread.** It is drawn on the same 390 × 560 page with a valley-fold spine down the middle (`placeholders.ts` `spine`). The crop of the photo is 300 × 225 page units inside the page, centred across on `photoRect`.
9. **Lab stage.** `lab-spread` is 1-2 as a spread (index 6, the bead renamed `drumstick`, `photoRect` round the right platform: the bench is in, the pillar is out). `SPREAD_REGISTRY` puts it after 1-2 so the shell can play it (`spread-shell`).

**Why.** A viewfinder that shows the shot it will take makes the timing readable without explaining frames. It needs no change to the paused page. Saving every shot is the simplest reading of "the last photo taken is kept" that never loses a photo to a closed tab.

**Consequences.**
- #107 registers 1-6 with `spread` and `photoRect`. Then it moves `photo-moment`, `spread-c1` and the `photo-spread` play scenario onto `c1p6`, and wires `c1p6.end.*` after the album.
- The character artist: the photo frames A, B and C (+M) per chapter can replace the composites in `PhotoScene` without changing anything else.
- #113 adds the album's chapter navigation and the 淘氣大王 cover (`rewardFlags(save).mischiefCover`).
- #105: the shutter click, the flash, the note pops (`[data-album-note]`).
- New UI strings not in § 5.7: 「寶咖咖一歲的相簿」 (§ 2.9's name), 學會了 (D-62's), 返回, and 已收集 as the grid's accessible name.

### D-67 · 2026-10-19 · Settings, 長輩模式 and 大字 (#104)

**Context.** #104 builds GDD § 2.11: every setting, 長輩模式 (one switch that sets several things), 「重新開始」 behind a 3-second hold, and the modes of bible § 9.3 (the 長輩模式 extras, the optional gyro tilt, low power). The docs leave open:
- how 長輩模式 "restores the individual settings the player had" (#93's note suggested applying its effects as a patch);
- whether its 15 s auto-hint holds when the player had 自動提示 off (#100's `autoHintMs` kept 關);
- what "hold-to-show-parts always on" means;
- where the tilt setting lives in the save (§ 3.8 lists no such field);
- how 大字 reaches every screen, and what happens where the larger text no longer fits.

**Decision.**
1. **長輩模式 is a view, never a write.** `Settings` keeps the player's own 速度, 字的大小 and 自動提示; `effectiveSettings` (`game/settings.ts`) reads 慢, 大字 and 15 秒 over them while `elder` is on. Turning it off restores them with nothing to snapshot. While it is on, a change to a held setting is dropped (`elderPatch`, inside `updateSettings`), and the settings screen shows those rows locked with a padlock and 「長輩模式幫你設好了」. The tilt row is locked off too.
2. **15 秒 always in 長輩模式**, even over 關 (supersedes D-63's "unless it is off"): the GDD says "auto-hint at 15 s", and a held row must show one value.
3. **Hold-to-show-parts always on** = the hold-to-reveal outlines (§ 2.6) are drawn all the time in 長輩模式, no hold needed. The other extras: perch markers bolder (a 1.4× paw with an ink edge on a stronger halo), the 28 px seals (#140's `sealR`), and #140's stronger L1 halo — all through `affordance.elder`.
4. **The tilt** is `Settings.tilt`, optional so a save from before #104 still loads (the guard accepts it missing; § 3.8 is otherwise unchanged). Its row is 「書本傾斜」. Off by default; iOS is asked for the gyro on the tap that turns it on. It runs only when not in 長輩模式, reduced motion or low power. `useTilt` leans the whole page box, at most ±3°; per-layer parallax is left to #136.
5. **Low power** latches for the session when the last 2 s of frames average over 20 ms *and* most of them are over 20 ms (a sustained slowdown, not one stall); a gap over 250 ms starts the measure over. It drops the tilt and the turn's confetti and halves the grab's sparkle burst. It is not saved.
6. **震動**: `navigator.vibrate` pulses of 12 ms (a drag's snap), 22 ms (Mocha's push) and 16-60-16 ms (a sticker: earned mid-page, and the sticker card's slap).
7. **大字 is +25 % everywhere.** The shell provides `TextScaleContext` (1 or 1.25) and sets `[data-text-size="big"]` on its root and on `<html>` (the pause menu's sheet is portalled). Text sized in code multiplies by the scale. HTML text sized with Tailwind's `text-[Npx]` grows by `textSize.css`, except display type of 44 px and up. Where the larger text would not fit:
   - the HUD's clock chip grows with its digits and the chapter line is condensed with `textLength`;
   - the chapter card scales with the screen's width (up to 1.25) and holds its smaller words at 24 px;
   - on a small book (bubbles grown past 1.4×, e.g. 375 × 667) a subtitle beat holds one line instead of 3, and the chapter card's beats hold 2;
   - three-glyph words in the word ring are 24 px, to stay on the disc's cream inside its rim;
   - the album's buttons put the icon over the words.
8. **The settings screen** is cream index cards on the 花磚 endpaper: 長輩模式 first, alone, with a one-line summary, then 速度 / 字的大小 / 自動提示, the two volume sliders, and 震動 / 減少動態 / 左手模式 / 書本傾斜. Every choice is a radio group of paper buttons: the chosen one is navy with a ✓ (a shape as well as a colour), and the rest are cream. 減少動態 shows the system's value with 「跟著手機的設定」 until the player picks one. 「重新開始（按住三秒）」 shows a countdown and a red bar while held; letting go cancels it. When it completes, the game is new (settings kept, D-55) and the cover opens.
9. **New UI strings not in § 5.7:** 書本傾斜, the switches' 關／開, 「慢一點、字大一點、提示快一點」, 「長輩模式幫你設好了」, 「跟著手機的設定」, 返回 and %. The stub screen and its strings are gone.

**Why.** A derived view makes "turning it off restores what you had" true by construction, and a reload in the middle cannot break it. Growing every text by one factor, with each screen fitted case by case, keeps 大字 honest. `check:ui:big` proves it on every fixture.

**Consequences.**
- `npm run check:ui:big` (`UI_TEXT=big`) opens every popup fixture with 大字 on; it passes today. New screens should pass it too (handbook § 5). `#/pagelab?fixture=<name>&text=big` shows any fixture with 大字.
- `check:ui` no longer measures text clipped away by a scroller.
- #105 reads `sfx` and `music` (0–100) from the save for its volumes.
- #136: per-layer tilt parallax, and a device pass on low power and vibration.

### D-68 · 2026-10-19 · Sound: the cue map, the music box and where sound lives (#105)

**Context.** #105 extends `src/shared/audio.ts` with GDD § 2.12's cues, the barks as voice blips and a music-box loop for chapters 0 and 1, with the settings' volumes and the iOS unlock. The docs leave open:
- where the sounds are triggered from, and how to keep the dev tools and the visual QA silent;
- which event sounds a part's move (the gesture on release, or the tick that applies it a moment later);
- the material of a pushed thing's drop and the voice of a creature (§ 2.12 names examples, the page data has no field);
- how the turn's and the cards' sounds keep time with their animations (a hold, a skip);
- what plays on the cover and on chapters without a loop yet;
- how loud 0–100 is.

**Decision.**
1. **One synth, one map.** `shared/audio.ts` only synthesises: voices (an oscillator note or a filtered-noise burst, each with a click-free exponential envelope) on two buses. `popup/view/sound.ts` decides what sounds: `tickSounds(def, prev, state, events, moved)`, `gestureSounds`, `stickerSounds` — pure functions, tested row by row against § 2.12.
2. **The shell is the only live sink.** `GameShell` provides `SoundContext` (`LIVE`); its default is `SILENT`. So the art lab, PageLab, `#/popup?page=` and every still never make a sound, with no flags to pass. The shell also unlocks audio on the first `pointerdown`, `pointerup`, `touchend` or `keydown` (capture phase; on iOS a touch only counts as a user activation as it ends), sets the volumes live and suspends the context while the tab is hidden.
3. **A part sounds on release.** A drag's snap, or a tap on a part, sounds at once (click / ratchet / flop by preset); the tick that applies it skips that part's `partEnter` (`moved`, 1.5 s). A part moved by the world (a reaction, Mocha's weight, an emit) sounds on its tick. A wheel also ratchets at every stop it passes under the finger. A drag start swishes (not a wheel: it ratchets).
4. **What sounds from a tick:** the word said (its motif), the target change (ding, then the new target's bark as voice blips 0.24 s later), the barks as `barksOf` chooses them (「要！要！」 the blip-blip, 哇—— the soft cry glide, the rest one voice blip per syllable; Mocha's 「喵？」 a mew, 「不要。」 a puff, 「……」 silent), looking (the up-glide), each crawl step (pat-pat, not while carried or riding), each of Mocha's nodes (a thump, heavier as he lands), a push (scrape) and a `land` (the drop), a creature's position change (its voice), a grab (the sparkle), `locked` / `jam` (the boop).
5. **Materials and voices come from the art key** until a page needs more: a pushable whose art says coin / token clinks, pigeon / bird flaps, the rest is paper; a creature that is a pigeon coos, a goose or duck honks, a squirrel / mouse / monkey chitters, the rest chirp.
6. **Animations time their own sounds.** The turn takes a `sound` hook and plays its cues as steps on its own clock (fold, leaf = whoosh + chime, fwump at the new pop-ups' overshoot, the card's drop, flaps and lift), so a hold waits and a skip stops them; the new page's fwump in a fold or chapter turn is set off at the swap with a delay, because its rise goes on under the card. The sticker card's slaps, the album's print and notes run on the screen's timers, cleared when it goes. Reduced motion keeps the sounds (one leaf for the cross-fade; no card drop).
7. **Music.** The cover and the prologue play `chapter0` (an 8-bar lullaby in G, 0.42 s steps), chapter 1 `chapter1` (12 bars in C, 0.28 s). A chapter with no loop yet is silent (each chapter issue adds its own). The loop changes at the turn's swap with a 0.35 s fade. 音樂 0 stops the scheduler.
8. **Volume** is squared: 0–100 → 0–1 gain (`volumeGain`), so the slider's middle sounds like the middle. Defaults 80 / 50 put the music box at about a quarter of the effects' peaks. Changing 音效 plays a click at the new level.
9. **Dev hook.** In dev builds `LIVE` also pushes every sound to `window.__popupSounds` when a script set it up before load (QA, the probe used for this issue); production strips it.

**Why.** A pure map is testable without WebAudio and keeps R9 honest (every cue is attached to a visual change it reads from). Making silence the default means no screenshot or lab view can ever start a sound. Sounding the player's own move on release, not a tick later, is what makes paper feel under the finger.

**Consequences.**
- Chapters 2–10: add a `chapterN` loop to `TRACKS` and `chapterTrack`.
- New creatures and pushables: check `creatureCue` / `dropCue` pick the right voice from their art key, or add a keyword.
- #111 prunes the RPG's tones and tracks.
- The "record the parents' voices" stretch idea stays with the owner.
- The bundle grew ≈ 4.5 KB gzip (all JS chunks), under the 6 KB budget.

### D-69 · 2026-10-19 · The prologue pages and their scripted tutorials (#106)

**Context.** #106 builds 0-2, 0-3 and 0-4 from GDD § 4.3 and the prologue's scripted tutorials. The docs leave open:
- what "first play only (flag in the save)" reads;
- how a tutorial is told apart from the hint ladder, and when its clock runs;
- what "the tutorial highlights the bubble" looks like;
- where the pages' unscripted lines come from (Dad's sleep-talk on 0-2, 寶咖咖's pat on 0-3's 紅包);
- how the placeholder stage draws a curtain rod, a shelf and a laundry basket before #108 bakes the art;
- the lab stages #95, #141 and #98 left for these pages.

**Decision.**
1. **First play = not solved yet.** The shell passes `tutorials` to `PagePlayer` while `save.pages[id].solved` is unset, so the save's existing record is the flag and the save format doesn't change. A player who leaves or retries before solving the page sees the tutorial again, which is what a grandparent needs. `#/popup?page=…&tutorial=1` turns it on outside the shell; PageLab and stills never run it.
2. **Tutorials are scripts, not hints.** `game/tutorial.ts` is a table by page id: a situation predicate on the state, what to show, and a delay. `PagePlayer` keeps the clock. The cue must hold for its delay while the page is live: not paused, not held by a layer or a turn, no word ring, card or photo up, and no subtitle on screen (0-3's join lines come first). Any change restarts the clock. A tutorial uses the ladder's visuals (`affordance.hint` level 2: the ghost hand with its halo). The ladder's own hint wins when one is showing.
   - 0-1: `crib-rail` (drag to `down`) after 4 s of reaching with the rail up.
   - 0-2: the bubble spotlight 1.2 s after he starts watching the TV, kept through the flip until he has the bead.
   - 0-3: `perch:table` after 4 s of reaching for the bead while Mocha is neither on the table nor on his way there. The hand stays if he is sent to the shelf instead.
3. **The bubble spotlight is moonlight, not marigold.** The marigold halo means "touch this" (bible § 4), and the bubble isn't a touchable. The spotlight is a soft lamp glow and a dotted chalk ring round the cloud. It breathes at 0.5 Hz (opacity only) and holds still under reduced motion. It is `affordance.bubble`.
4. **Two lines added to the script:** `c0p2.dad.1` 爸爸 「叫……爸爸……」 (the newspaper opened, § 4.3's 淘氣) and `c0p3.envelope.1` 寶咖咖 「這個！」 (the 紅包 lands). The 紅包 is flavour only: it is no attractor, so he keeps wanting the bead. Mocha's join lines are 0-3's `lines.intro`. 廣播姊姊's three lines are 0-4's `lines.onEvent` on `partEnter train on`.
5. **Prologue 土地公 wear no hat** (as 0-1), though the engine fixture's 0-4 gave him a 斗笠.
6. **The placeholder stage indoors.** A perch on the standing page (above the gutter) is a wall shelf (a plank on two brackets), not a floor pillar or a bench. A `hidesMocha` perch is a woven laundry basket with its rim at the perch node, so Mocha's in-box frame sits on it (#141's note), with a towel over the front edge. A node attached to a gate is floor (a doorway never moves, L04), not a carriage. Backdrops: `c0/living-room` (0-2, 0-3) and `c0/home-window` (0-4) bake the chapter's placeholder stage until #108 draws them.
7. **The lab stages are gone.** `lab-push`, `lab-cry`, `lab-lamp` and `lab-box` are replaced by real fixtures: `c0p2-watching`, `c0p2-change-of-mind`, `c0p3-eye-contact`, `c0p4-mocha-inbox` (looking too) and `c0p4-crying`, plus `c0p2-start`, `c0p2-bubble-tutorial`, `c0p3-join`, `c0p3-tutorial`, `c0p3-envelope`, `c0p4-start` and `c0p4-train`.

**Why.** The flag the save already has says exactly "has this player solved it", so a new field would only duplicate it. Scripts keep the tutorials exactly as the GDD writes them and separate from the solver-driven ladder. A delay on a held situation gives the player the moment to find it alone, which is the point of a tutorial.

**Consequences.**
- 1-3's word tutorial (#99) stays where it is (`game/words.ts`). A later page that needs a scripted beat adds a row to `SCRIPTS`.
- The screen fixtures' "played" save now solves the whole prologue before 1-2, since 1-2 unlocks only after 0-4.
- #108 replaces the placeholder backdrops and the shelf and basket furniture with the prologue's art. Place the basket's rim at the `basket` perch node (0-4: (34, 420)).

### D-70 · 2026-10-19 · Chapter 1's pages, the chapter's end and riding parts (#107)

**Context.** #107 builds 1-1 and 1-3…1-6 from GDD § 4.3 and the chapter-end flow (album → end lines → the tray's first slot → the next chapter's card, a 「未完待續」 card until chapter 2 exists). Building them showed:
- 1-1 as written puts four touchables plus perches on a chapter's first page (bible § 4.2 allows three), and its sway is a "prop pull-tab", which no preset is;
- 1-3's 淘氣 (say 嗶！ to the robot) comes free with the solution, because his 嗶！ at gate B always reaches it;
- 1-5's par is 2, not 3, and its 淘氣 (Mocha rides the elevator three times without him) can't happen: he boards the car the tick it reaches B1, and the next ride up ends the page;
- 1-6's lid sits on a sliding cart, but parts are drawn and touched at a fixed `hit`; the pigeon on the cart node left him one edge from the 雞腿, so the photo moment always came after the grab;
- the docs don't say how the end lines show, how the tray "fills", or what the 「未完待續」 card looks like.

**Decision.**
1. **1-1** has two hand straps (Mocha on the first; the second and the rack carry paw prints) plus the `sway` tab: three. `sway` is a `tab` (`left` / `level` / `right`), a pure toy. The page opens on 廣播姊姊's ride lines `rec.pa.ride.1`, `.2` (the card said 「下一站，台北車站」).
2. **1-3's 淘氣** is 「對載著摩卡的掃地機器人說『嗶！』」: a `beep` heard while Mocha rides the spinning robot (a new perch, `robotLid`, a jump from the counter). The robot still spins at every 嗶！ in reach, so everyone sees it in the solution; the sticker needs the cat on board.
3. **1-5's 淘氣** is Mocha's: he sits on the shop counter's 叮咚 bell (`onPart`, the bell starts `down`). Tapping him (`pet`) rings it again; the first ring plays the 店員小哥 cameo (`c1p5.clerk.1`–`3`), and three rings are 「讓摩卡按三次叮咚鈴」. It also shows "tap Mocha to do it again" before the elevator needs it. Par 2 is written back to the GDD.
4. **1-4's 淘氣** counts four turns with him anywhere on the disk (its centre or an aisle seat). The solution says 嗶！ before the turn back, so each gesture starts from its own part states (the hint fallback, #100, matches on them).
5. **1-6.** The cart carries a step (`cartStep`, where the pigeon lands) and the bento (`K`, the 雞腿): he reaches two edges out, so the photo moment begins on his approach. With Mocha on the awning the pigeon ignores the lid. The cart starts at `middle`, so the 土地公 at `kitchen` is a find.
6. **Riding parts.** `PartDef.ridesOn?: Id` names a node the part sits on. Its `hit` and art are authored where the node is in the initial state, and the view moves both with the node (`gestures.ts` `rideOffset` / `rideHome`; `PageView` translates the part; the flap's halo and ghost hand follow the moved hit). The simulation never reads it: event origins use the authored hit. 1-6's lid rides the cart; 1-5's panel rides the car.
7. **The placeholder stage.** A perch on a node a moving part carries gets no post (the part's art holds it), and a carriage carries only the nodes Baokaka walks (the cart's awning perch no longer stops its card being drawn).
8. **The chapter's end** (`GameShell`): after 「放進相簿」 the end lines (`content/chapters.ts` `endLines`) play as a modal over the dimmed spread, a tap a beat, on the chapter card's line cards. The HUD holds the new coin back (`trayHold`) until then; then it drops into its slot with one foil flare (`TopHud` `fill`; a fade under reduced motion), with the coin and sparkle sounds, and holds 1.3 s. Then the sticker card and the chapter turn. After the book's last built chapter the turn shows the next chapter's card with 「未完待續」 (`UI.chapterCard.soon`) on its split-flap row and `rec.soon.1` 「下一站還在畫……未完待續。」 under it, then the 目錄. A replayed spread plays the end lines again, with no fill.
9. **The thought bubble** draws a spread's treasure itself (`treasureArt`, `data-bubble-treasure`), not the bead.
10. **The lab stages are gone.** `lab-gates` and `lab-spread` (and `SPREAD_REGISTRY`) are replaced by the real pages: the `word-*` fixtures are on `c1p3`; `spread-c1`, `photo-moment` and `spread-shell` are on `c1p6`; `check:play` plays `c1p3` and `photo-spread` on the real pages. `lab-words` (every word at once) stays, now on `c1p3`.

**Why.** Each change keeps the GDD's puzzle and aha, and makes the 淘氣 a deliberate act (it is a reward for playing around, not for solving). Riding is the smallest generic answer to "a thing on a moving thing": data, one view transform and a hit shift, with no change to the core. Holding the coin back until the lines are read makes the tray's slot fill in front of the player, as the GDD asks.

**Consequences.**
- Saves and fixtures that start a player "at 1-2" now solve 1-1 too, since 1-2 unlocks after it.
- #108 draws chapter 1's art for these keys: backdrops `c1/car`, `c1/gates`, `c1/hall`, `c1/bento-shop`, `c1/bento-spread` (the placeholder stage until then); parts `c1/map`, `c1/sway`, `c1/card`, `c1/reader`, `c1/gate`, `c1/robot`, `c1/turntable`, `c1/elevator`, `c1/elevator-panel`, `c1/counter-bell`, `c1/bento-cart`, `c1/bento-lid`; creature `c1/backpacker`. The cart's awning and the car's panel need drawing on their parts (no perch post is drawn for them).
- Chapters 2–10 add `endLines` rows; the 「未完待續」 card moves on as each chapter lands.

### D-71 · 2026-10-20 · Slice art: the prologue's pages and how page art dresses the live stage (#108)

**Context.** #108 replaces the placeholder stage with real art for the ten slice pages (minimum 0-1, 1-2 and the cover). The bake (#96) turns backdrops and parts into bitmaps, but much of what a page shows is live and derived from the walk graph: every crawlable run is a paper ledge, every perch gets a pillar, bench, shelf or basket, and a potted plant fills a near corner. Real art needs that stage to match it: a crib is not a rug box, a hand strap is not a pillar, the 抓周 table is drawn in the backdrop. The issue also asks for creatures and cast (the pigeon, the backpacker), the 土地公's chapter hat, the squint test's calm lights, and touchables that keep the four cues.

**Decision.**
1. **Stage dress** (`view/art/dress.ts`): a small runtime table keyed by backdrop art key names what the stage is made of on that backdrop — the ledges' material (`playmat` 巧拼 foam tiles, the `crib`'s slatted side, `carBody`, `granite`, `shopFloor`, `balcony`, or the default `brick` / `rug`, per run by its feet line), an optional `ledgeSplit` (0-4: the mat stops at the balcony door), the perches whose furniture the backdrop draws itself (`ownPosts`: the stage then draws no post), and the corner card (`plant`, `blocks`, `luggage` or none). The dressed ledges and cards are live flat SVG (`view/art/ledges.ts`), drawn with few nodes (one path per colour).
2. **Prologue art** (`env/art/c0.ts`, shared helpers in `env/art/common.ts`): four backdrops — `c0/nursery-night` (0-1), `c0/living-room` (0-2), a new `c0/zhuazhou-corner` for 0-3 (its 抓周 table, shelf and calendar sit where 0-2's sofa and TV are, so the two pages can't share one drawing; 0-3's `backdrop` changes), `c0/home-window` (0-4) — and nine baked parts: the mobile (prop), the TV and the moon train (lights), the balcony door (gate), the 紅包 and the bead (pushables), and three **baked flaps** (newspaper, cushion, curtain).
3. **Baked flaps** keep the grandparent rule: the source draws the printed face (`closed`) and its back (`open`) in the card's shape with the **marigold dog-ear** in its kit place (the one marigold it wears; the source is linted in `env.test.ts` with the ear removed), local origin at the card's top-left, hinge 0. `FlapView` keeps the 翻 seal live and now also draws the printed curl cue live over a baked face.
4. **Creatures and cast** are live (`view/art/creatures.ts`): an origami pigeon, and the paper-style backpacker as a die-cut figure (flat cut-paper colour inside the standee's warm-white card and cut line). `CreatureArt` takes the creature's position, so the backpacker is drawn asleep against his backpack or sat up awake. Carriages have art per part key (`view/art/carriages.ts`): 1-2's cream 台鐵 train, 1-6's bento cart with its awning.
5. **The 土地公 wears his chapter's hat**: `tudigong(ctx, hat)` draws the prologue's red 員外帽 or chapter 1's navy 站務帽 with a foil badge (`PageDef.tudigong.hat`).
6. **Calm lights** (#140's squint note): the moon is a smaller disc with one small halo and a vellum cloud over it; lamp glows are kept small; the play-mat's pastels are dusky. Mocha asleep in the background (GDD § 4.3) is a printed paper cat in the backdrop, not a sprite; so are 爸爸 asleep (his face under the newspaper flap), the family photo and 咻咻's peeking cloud.
7. 0-4 lists the train before the curtain so the curtain's grip ear draws over the train's roof.

**Why.** The table keeps art decisions with the art (a backdrop knows what its ledges are) without new page data or engine changes, and it degrades to the placeholder stage for any key without an entry. Live dressed ledges stay correct for every part state (a ledge is drawn from the graph), while the bitmap carries the heavy painting. Baking a flap's face but keeping its seal and cue live keeps the affordance lint meaningful.

**Consequences.**
- A backdrop that draws a perch's furniture must list it in `ownPosts`; `dress.test.ts` checks every owned post names a real perch on a page using that backdrop.
- Chapter 1's dress entries land with chapter 1's backdrops (the second #108 PR), so no page loses its furniture before its art exists.
- The carriage and creature art count toward the animated-node budget (150): 1-6 sits at the limit, so new moving art there must stay lean.
- **Chapter 1 (PR B).** The six station backdrops and eleven parts follow the same rules. The turntable is a baked **wheel**: its knurled rim is the marigold grip (`data-grip="rim"`, linted like a flap's ear), and its seal and turn cue stay live. Each state's bitmap is drawn unturned, because the view rotates it. The 土地公 is printed into state 2 upside down, since a live 土地公 at a node on the disk would sit under it. The 1-2 train's `hit` now starts at its card's left end (x 28; the engine fixture and GDD § 3.4 follow), and chapter 1's pages all give him the 站務帽.

### D-72 · 2026-10-20 · The routing switch (#109)

**Context.** #109 makes 翻翻台北 the site's game. GDD § 3.5 and § 7 item 24 say the RPG stays in the repo, unrouted, until the owner signs off after the M1 playtest (#111 deletes it). The issue left how to load the game, how the farewell looks and what the deployment checks measure open.

**Decision.**
1. **Routes.** `#/`, `#/popup`, `#/quest` and any unknown hash show 翻翻台北; `#/find` and `#/sudoku` still rewrite to `#/`. `popup` is no longer a route of its own (`Route` is `game | legacy | artlab | pagelab`); the game reads its dev parameters from any game hash, so `#/?page=c1p2` and `#/popup?page=c1p2` both work.
2. **One main chunk.** `App` imports `PopupGame` statically: the cover is what everyone opens, so a lazy chunk would only add a round trip. Nothing in a production chunk imports `src/quest/` (0 of 513 modules; the art lab, the only importer left, is dev-only). Main chunk: 584 KB (191 KB gzip), against 630 KB RPG + 420 KB game before.
3. **No RPG chrome.** `<ArtDefs/>` (the RPG sprites' gradients) mounts only around the art lab, and the RPG's `OrientationGuard` is gone from the game (its navy kit panel belongs to the RPG; a pop-up-style landscape notice is a follow-up if the playtest asks for it).
4. **The farewell.** `main.tsx` calls `retireRpgSave()` at boot (after `cleanupLegacyStorage`); `App` → `PopupGame` → `GameShell` pass the result on, and the cover shows `RPG_FAREWELL` 「寶咖咖的新冒險開始了！」 on a cream index card taped with pink washi, as wide as the buttons, above 「開始」 / 「繼續」. It stays on the cover until the player first opens a page. The next boot finds no RPG keys, so it is said once. It is a `role="status"` note, not a button.
5. **Site metadata.** `<title>` and `og:title` 《翻翻台北》Pop-Up Taipei; the description, `og:description` and the manifest describe the pop-up book; manifest `name` 「翻翻台北 Pop-Up Taipei」, `short_name` 「翻翻台北」; `theme-color` / `background_color` are 阿嬤's quilt `#3A1838` (the cover's ground) instead of the RPG's navy. The icons stay (they have no text). The share card is a new `icons/og.jpg` (1200 × 630, 99 KB): the cover scene with both standees on the quilt, and a taped card with the title, the hook 「寶寶不聽指揮——你只能改變台北。」 and 「拉紙片、轉轉盤、掀紙蓋的立體書解謎」. The old `og.png` (the mini-game era's 「找出摩卡貓藏起來的東西」) is deleted.
6. **Deployment checks.** `npm run check:prod` (`scripts/prod-check.mjs`) is the production smoke test; its load numbers are a report, not a gate, as the issue asks. Throttled 4G means WebPageTest's "4G" profile (9 Mbps both ways, 170 ms RTT) through CDP with the cache off.

**Why.** The game is the only thing the site serves now, so it should load as one piece; the farewell is the moment an old player loses their save, so it is paper and warm, in the book's own style, and never technical.

**Consequences.**
- #111 deletes `src/quest/`, the art lab, `ArtDefs`' RPG use and the RPG baselines; the route table needs no change then (`artlab` goes with the lab).
- The `title` visual target is now the cover on first launch (`#/`, waiting for `[data-screen-ready]`).
- Tailwind still scans `src/quest/` (the art lab needs its classes in dev), so the CSS keeps the RPG's utilities until #111.


### D-73 · 2026-10-20 · The M1 playtest kit and the on-device log (#110)

**Context.** #110 asks for a playtest kit (a run guide, an observation sheet, a metrics log at `#/popup?debug=metrics`) and rules out analytics of any kind. GDD § 8 says the M1 gates are observed, not instrumented. The issue left open where the log lives, what "time per page" means, and how the kit reaches the family (who read 繁體中文).

**Decision.**
1. **The log is on in every build, behind the parameter.** The playtest runs on the live site (#109), so `debug=metrics` works in production: `#/?debug=metrics` (and `#/popup?debug=metrics`). Without the parameter nothing is recorded and no code path writes. With it, the shell appends events to `popup.playtest.v1` in `localStorage` (capped at 6000) and shows a small chip at the top. The chip has a ★ to mark a moment and opens a panel to copy, save (`.md`) or clear the report. Nothing leaves the phone.
2. **Active time, not wall time.** A page's time is the time it is live: open, not held by a layer (the 選單 / 目錄 / 設定, the log panel), not in a turn or the chapter card, not behind the sticker card or album, and the tab visible. Reading the page's own lines counts; the card's don't. An attempt runs from the open to the solve; 重來 continues it, and opening another page ends it unsolved.
3. **What is recorded** (no names, no content typed by anyone): page opens (and whether the page was already solved, a replay), live on/off, the player's own moves, taps on locked parts, taps on Baokaka, hints shown with their level (0 = the auto nudge), 重來, mid-page stickers, the solve with its move count, and the observer's ★ marks.
4. **The report reads only what the device can measure.** It shows 0-1's time and whether it was solved with no 提示 / 重來, the prologue's total and its 提示 presses, chapter 1 pages that reached level 3, chapter 1's median, "stuck" stretches (over 2 min with no move and no 提示 press, on a page with no 提示) and replays. The observed gates (unaided, laughs, predictions, the child's rule) stay on the sheet.
5. **The kit** is `docs/playtest/`. The observer's guide and the sheet are in English, as the repo's docs are. The page the testers read (`guide-zh.md`) and the owner's summary (`owner-guide.md`) are in 繁體中文. `ai-playtest.md` records an automated first-time run on the production build; it lists things to watch, not results. `m1-results.md` is a template until the session.
6. **No fixture or visual baseline for the chip.** It is an observer's tool, its text is a live clock, and it never shows without the parameter. It is covered by `playtest.test.ts` and the kit's screenshots.

**Why.** An in-memory, opt-in log answers "how long, how many hints" exactly. That spares the observer a stopwatch, so they can watch faces. Keeping it on the device and off by default stays inside the issue's "no analytics" rule.

**Consequences.**
- #110 stays open until the session: `m1-results.md` is filled, every failed gate gets an issue on the roadmap, and the owner's go/no-go is recorded here as a new decision. That unblocks #111, #112 and #113.
- The chip is 22 px tall (below R6's 48 px): it is not part of the game's UI. It sits flush with the top so it never covers the HUD's title.
- New shell layers that hold the page should also count as "not live" (`live` in `GameShell`).

### D-74 · 2026-10-20 · Fixing the AI playtest's friction before the family session (#183)

**Context.** The automated first-time run on #110 (`docs/playtest/ai-playtest.md`) listed twelve friction points. The owner's family playtest should test the design, not known problems, so the clear ones are fixed first. Each fix had a choice to make.

**Decision.**
1. **A tap anywhere reads the lines on.** While a subtitle beat is up, a pointer that lands on the page and lifts as a tap is cancelled (no input, nothing pressed, the world resumes) and the beat moves on. A pointer that turns into a drag plays on the page as usual and the line stays: event lines (Mocha's translations) arrive mid-play, and eating a drag there felt worse than a line staying up. A gesture already under way when a line arrives is never a reader. The bar, the ring, the word card and the photo moment keep their taps.
2. **Paws are never hidden.** Mocha's perch paws, and the hint halo of a perch, draw in the `fx` layer above the standees, the thought bubble and the barks; only a subtitle may lie over one (any tap clears it). The paw gained an ink hairline and a stronger halo (it vanished on 1-1's cream map). Every bubble now keeps off the paws and the hinted target as a soft avoid, and 廣播姊姊's lines may drop down the page. Guards: affordance lint A08 (drawing order, unit tests over every page's solution with the L1 hint on) and `check:ui`'s hit test of every paw and halo on every fixture.
3. **Soft traps fail a page.** The solver reports a *soft trap*: a first move of a part or of Mocha, from a state on a shortest way to the goal, after which the goal costs ≥ 2 more moves (it must be undone). Moving again something already moved on the way (undoing progress) is a wasted move, not a trap. On the slice this finds exactly 0-4's curtain-first trap and nothing else. `pages.test.ts` fails on any; `npm run solve` has a `traps` column.
4. **0-4: the bead glints only once the train is lit.** Its `visibleWhen` is curtain open **and** train on. Opened first, the curtain shows a dark train and nothing to want, so hiding Mocha still makes him cry; the cry lights the train and its bead. Either order solves in 2; the aha (crying is a tool) is kept. The GDD's "most players open the curtain first, then learn to hide everything" is dropped: the AI run showed that state reads as a broken page, not a lesson.
5. **1-1's third strap is looped up** (a dark belt with a triangular grip, no steel ring), so only the perch straps look like perches.
6. **1-5 feedback.** A tap on a perch Mocha can't reach now does what GDD § 2.3 always said: the paw shakes and he turns to look at it, with his existing 「喵？」 (kept over 「不要。」, which § 5.3 reserves for water). After the car comes down with them in it, a **first-play nudge** (`game/tutorial.ts`, a new `nudge` kind): after 4 s Mocha taps his paw on the panel, shimmers once (the L0 look) and asks 「再按一次？」 (`content/hints.ts` `NUDGE_LINES`). It points at him without playing the answer.
7. **No line under the photo moment**, and a bubble riding a character never leaves the page (it is cut at the page's edges): a long trip held mid-way left one under the HUD.
8. **Cheap extras:** a bobbing ▼ on the chapter card's lines and the word card (they wait for a tap), and a creature at rest beside a shiny faces it (1-6's pigeon looked away from the 雞腿).

**Left for the human playtest:** the reading load at the start (#2), the backpacker's punchline timing (#7) and the L2 turn hint (#9). `ai-playtest.md` records the status of each finding.

**Consequences.** New pages must have no soft traps. Scenarios in `check:play` that tap on through a line now read it first. A scripted nudge may carry one short Mocha line.

### D-75 · 2026-10-20 · The M2 engine: forgetting a refusal, walkable decoys, ties mid-page, the toy pages (#112)

**Context.** #112 completes the M2 engine: spotlights (`nodeBy`), creatures holding the goal (`heldBy`, 掰掰's `drop`), decoys and the words 掰掰 / 不要！ / 哇！. Most of the mechanics were already in the core (#82–#86, D-43, D-45); what the docs left open was how 不要！ avoids the soft-lock D-46 (11) found (he refuses the only thing he wants and cries forever, so every page from 3-3 on that offers 不要！ would be a dead end), how "a decoy's node is unreachable" is verified, and what "visible-tie warning updated" means once targets move mid-page.

**Decision.**
1. **A refusal is forgotten after two cries.** Crying because of his own refusals, `baby.lookCount` counts the cries; on the second (`FORGET_AFTER_CRIES`) the refusals clear and next tick he takes the refused thing again (silently, as any comfort). Crying with nothing refused is unchanged (0-4's crying lasts until something appears), and `lookCount` stays 0 there, so no existing page's states change. Both cries go out, so "crying on demand" (§ 2.4) still works, and a reaction to `cry` happens on the first.
2. **Walkable decoys fail a page.** The solver checks every state it explores: a decoy whose current node (after `nodeBy`/`heldBy`/`followsPushable`) is in Baokaka's reach set at his growth is reported in `decoysReachable` (first state per decoy, with the input path). `judge` and `pages.test.ts` fail on any. The engine already treats decoys as unreachable (D-43), so this is about the screen: a decoy he could walk up to and not take would read as a bug. A decoy's kind isn't restricted (the GDD's are shinies).
3. **Visible ties in every reachable state.** `visibleTie(ranked)` (`baby.ts`) is the rule-14 test, shared by the lint (initial state, `L14-visible-tie`) and the solver (`visibleTies`, every non-done state, one entry per pair of ids). It is a **warning** in `npm run solve` and `pages.test.ts`: a spotlight aimed level with another light, or two shinies left level after a drop, would show it. No current page has one.
4. **`sayingWord`.** A say tick with no word event is 不要！ (the one word that emits nothing). It was read from a grown `refused` set, which missed 不要！ said while crying (nothing to refuse, e.g. 4-1's 淘氣) and every fixture still (no `prev`).
5. **Toy pages.** `core/fixtures/m2.ts` holds one complete page per behaviour, held to the real pages' bar in `core/m2.test.ts` (lint clean with no warnings, solution replays, complete solve, no dead ends, no soft traps, no walkable decoy, no ties, 淘氣 and 土地公 reachable, par as designed): `toy-spotlight` (2-2: a wheel aims a light attractor at a trap-door plate whose press raises the curtain — a `lift`, so the event-driven curtain shows no flap affordance), `toy-squirrel` (2-4: Mocha on the fork scares the squirrel down onto the bead's node, which it blocks; 掰掰 drops the bead and sends it off the page), `toy-moon` (3-3: a moon decoy; Mocha on the rock makes the goose honk, which teaches 不要！; refusing the moon sends him to Mocha past the nest, the goose leaves and the bead is out). Each has a "without the key move it is unsolvable" test. PageLab lists them, plus `lab-ring4` (the moon with M2's four words); fixtures `word-ring-4`, `word-card-no`, `word-say-no`, `m2-*`; `check:play` plays `m2-moon-no` and `m2-squirrel-bye` live.

**Why.** A baby who forgets what he refused after a short cry is true to life and keeps the "no failure" rule without asking every chapter-3+ page to design an escape; checking decoys and ties per reachable state is cheap (the slice's 10 pages solve in the same 0.3 s) and catches exactly what moving targets introduce.

**Consequences.**
- D-46 (11)'s workaround (`words: []` on toy pages) and its note that pages offering 不要！ "must give him another candidate or a way out of crying" no longer apply.
- #114/#116/#118 build chapter pages on these mechanics; their pages must pass the new decoy check, and should clear any `visibleTies` warning.
- View gaps left for those pages: a node attached to a button draws as a carriage on a track (a pressure plate needs its own look), a light attractor is a small star (a spotlight pool on the floor would read better), a held item sits on its creature's node, and the decoy draws as an ordinary bead.

### D-76 · 2026-10-20 · The whole album, its 淘氣大王 cover and the 目錄's 象山 postcard (#113)

**Context.** #103 built one chapter's album page and opened 相簿 on the latest chapter with a photo. #113 completes the album for every chapter (navigation, placeholders, the § 2.8 「淘氣大王」 cover) and the 目錄 for every chapter (the bonus 「象山的日出」 tile). The GDD says "swipe or tap arrows to flip pages" and that all 淘氣 unlock "the album's 「淘氣大王」 cover", but not where the cover lives, what a chapter not reached shows, or where the bonus tile goes.

**Decision.**
1. **The album is a book of leaves** (`game/album.ts`, pure): leaf 0 is the album's cover, leaves 1–10 the chapters. A chapter's page is `photo` (the kept photo, its notes, the word), `open` (reached, no photo yet: the empty print, as in #103) or `locked` (the 月亮線 hasn't reached it). A kept photo always shows, whatever is registered. The ending's 抓周 photo becomes leaf 11 with #135.
2. **Opening.** The cover's 相簿 opens at the latest chapter with a photo (D-66), or at the album's cover when there is none (it was chapter 1's empty page). The album layer can name a leaf (`{ kind: 'album', leaf }`; fixtures).
3. **A chapter not reached** is a placeholder that names the chapter (第N章, title, time: the 目錄 shows them too) but not its treasure or word: an empty print with a padlock and 「？」, a blind-embossed 「？」 for the word, the empty grid, and the 目錄's 「月亮線還沒開到這裡。」 where the notes go.
4. **Paging.** One leaf a turn, no wrap-around. The page arrows (56 px cream discs, faded and disabled at the ends) sit either side of the page dots in a row of their own above the buttons, so they are always in reach and never scroll away; a one-finger horizontal swipe across the leaf (≥ 48 px, 1.5× more across than down, ≤ 0.9 s; a second finger cancels; `touch-action: pan-y` keeps vertical scrolling) and ← / → do the same. The new leaf slides in from the side it was pulled from (a fade under reduced motion). The arrows do not mirror in 左手模式 (the direction is the book's); the buttons under them do, so the main one sits under the left thumb. Straight after a spread (`after` mode) there is no paging: that moment is the chapter's photo.
5. **The album's cover** is the navy linen board of bible § 7 with a gold-foil frame, a die-cut round window (the latest photo, else 寶咖咖's head under the moon) and 「寶咖咖 一歲的相簿」 in foil. **「淘氣大王」** crowns it: a foil crown on the window, a ring of foil 嘿 stickers, 寶咖咖 (laughing) and 摩卡 (smug) in the window and a seal-red ribbon reading 「淘氣大王」. It is read off the save (`rewardFlags`, D-64), never stored.
6. **「象山的日出」 on the 目錄** (once every 土地公 is found): a postcard taped onto the map's free corner (the serpentine's empty slot past 101; 象山 is 101's neighbour), a dotted gold branch from 101 to it. Tapped, the panel names it and, until #135 builds the page, says the 未完待續 line (`rec.soon.1`). Nav action `bonus`.
7. **Fixtures see the rewards** through `rewardTotal` (GameShell prop, fixture `screen.rewardTotal`): the number of pages the rewards need, set to the registered pages' count so `album-cover-unlocked` and `contents-all` show them today. The game uses 65 (`GAME_PAGES`).
8. **Strings.** 「淘氣大王」 (§ 2.8's name) is on the ribbon. Not in § 5.7, accessible names only: 上一頁 / 下一頁 (the arrows) and 封面 (the cover leaf).

**Why.** A cover leaf gives the 淘氣大王 a place without changing the game's cover or the album's one-tap path to the newest photo; placeholders make the album's size visible from the first photo (the long-term reward, § 2.9) without spoiling treasures; arrows in a fixed row keep R6/R7-style reach on a short phone while the leaf scrolls.

**Consequences.**
- #135 adds the ending's photo as the last leaf (`ALBUM_LEAVES`), credits over the album photos, and makes the 象山 postcard open the bonus page.
- Chapter issues (#114 …) get their album pages for free: a chapter's leaf opens as its first page is registered; its treasure art replaces the foil token (`treasureArt.ts`).

### D-77 · 2026-10-20 · Chapter 2's pages: specs in `docs/design/pages/`, the outline's changes, placeholder visuals for M2's mechanics (#114)

**Context.** #114 writes chapter 2 「會揮手的公園」 in full and builds 2-1 … 2-6 as data. The GDD's § 4.4 gives one row per page; the issue lets the full spec live in the GDD or in a linked page doc. Building the pages also showed where the outline and the M2 engine (#112, D-75) disagree, and #112's notes listed view gaps the pages would show (a pressure plate drawn as a carriage, a light as a star, a held item on the floor beside its creature).

**Decision.**
1. **Chapter specs live in `docs/design/pages/cN.md`**, linked from the chapter's § 4.4 heading, in the § 4.3 format plus a node/edge sketch and the solver's numbers. § 4.4's rows stay as the outline, edited where the spec changed them. (The GDD is long; one file per chapter keeps each chapter reviewable on its own.)
2. **Outline changes** (listed in `pages/c2.md`): 2-2 opens with Mocha in view, so hiding him in the 店員小哥's crate comes first (a light never beats Mocha), and the chapter's cameo plays there with Mocha's new line 「……又是你？」; 2-3's dog sleeps on the bead and Mocha is the first want, so the turtle's 淘氣 needs a trip back; 2-4's squirrel flees only when Mocha lands next to it; 2-5's 淘氣 becomes four egret rides on the pad, because a puff (`mochaPuff`) is a UI event and mischief predicates name § 3.2 events only (the C7 puff on the wet stone stays as the page's lesson; the stone is wet while the egret stamps on the pad, so L16 holds without an always-wet perch); 2-6's flaps are a bridge, a branch for Mocha and the portrait corner.
3. **The stored-solution hint fallback also matches creatures** (`core/hint.ts` `solutionAction`): it first looks for a solution gesture played from the same part states *and* creature positions, then part states alone, then the driven parts. Pages whose puzzle is a creature (2-4 has no parts; 2-5's egret) otherwise looped on the first gesture that still "did something" (sending the squirrel back up).
4. **Placeholder visuals for the M2 mechanics, until #115's art:** a node attached to a part that never moves it (a pressure plate) rides no carriage (`stage.ts`); a light attractor that follows a part (`nodeBy`) is a pool of lamplight on the floor under a faint beam (`spotPool`); a held item (`heldBy`, not yet dropped) is drawn smaller, up in its creature's paws. Four chapter 2 parts get real baked drawings now because their preset placeholder misleads (`env/art/c2.ts`): the umbrella (a pushable's default is a bead), the curtain (a lift's default is an elevator car), the trap-door plate and the lily-pad wheel. The 土地公's 斗笠 is drawn on the live placeholder. Creatures stay origami birds and backdrops the chapter's placeholder stage until #115.
5. **Chapter 2's music-box loop** (`chapter2`, 8 bars in D) is added with its pages, so the chapter is not silent.

**Why.** Each change keeps a page solver-green with no soft traps or free 淘氣, or keeps the chapter readable and playable before its art lands, without new engine features (an issue would be filed for `mochaPuff` predicates if a later page needs one).

**Consequences.**
- #116 and #118 put their specs in `docs/design/pages/c3.md` / `c4.md`.
- #115 replaces the placeholders above (and keeps the four baked parts' hit areas); the decoy look (a moon reflection, a bell) is still open for #116.
- `photo-spread` now runs from 1-6 into chapter 2's card and 2-1; `photo-spread-c2` plays 2-6 through chapter 3's 「未完待續」 card.

### D-78 · 2026-10-20 · Chapter 2's art: the park kit, pop-up flaps, conditional cut-outs, live fireflies (#115)

**Context.** #115 draws 大安森林公園 over #114's pages: six backdrops, the parts and cast the pages name, the 土地公's 掃把, the 畫筆 and the 淘氣 portrait of Mocha. Several things were left open: how the painted flaps on 2-6 "pop up into real paper" when the flap view only folds a card; how a drawing can appear with a state; how fireflies drift without a moving backdrop; what the stage draws at a crossing whose obstacle is the art itself; and the payload of a wheel whose four states are one drawing.

**Decision.**
1. **The palette kit** is `POPUP_PARK` (bible § 6 row 2) plus reusable cut-outs in `env/art/c2.ts`. New ledge styles (`parkPath`, `boardwalk`, `stage`, `carBodyXinyi`), plank ramps (`styledRamp`) and an arched bridge for a single-node park run; a `shrub` foreground card.
2. **Pop-up flaps** (`popFlap`): a flap's open bitmap is drawn through the inverse of `FlapView`'s fold (`scaleY −0.35` about the hinge), so the painted thing stands where it belongs on the page (a footbridge over the stream, a branch from the easel to the parasol) and grows out of the hinge as the card lifts. The kraft back and the marigold ear stay; the seal and cue stay live. No view change.
3. **`CutoutDef.visibleWhen`**: a cut-out shown only while a condition holds (2-6's portrait of Mocha, all three flaps open). Conditional cut-outs are drawn in the cut-outs layer, outside the static memo; they never move.
4. **`StageDress.crossing: 'none'`**: where the backdrop's art is the obstacle and a part is the way across (2-1's gap, 2-6's stream), the stage draws no rail.
5. **Fireflies drift live** (`StageDress.fireflies`, `view/Fireflies.tsx`): a few lime pinholes per page in the creatures layer, one node each on a CSS transform/opacity loop, counted in the animated budget; still under reduced motion and in screenshot stills. The backdrops also bake a field of still ones.
6. **A wheel whose states are one drawing bakes one bitmap** (the lily pad lists one state: the loader draws the first listed for any other). 2-5 went from 296 KB to 250 KB.
7. **A run wholly past a dress's `ledgeSplit` takes the split's material** (it took the main one). No existing page had such a run.
8. **People on the page drop one shadow** (`figure` in `view/art/park.ts`) instead of `depth`'s four copies: the die-cut card is their cut edge. This keeps 2-6 (two figures, three flaps) within 150 animated nodes (149 at its busiest tick).
9. **Not drawn:** the photo frames stay #103's composites (D-66, as chapter 1's do), now with the 畫筆; the chapter card has no landmark slot (it hangs over 2-1's new art) and the 目錄's page strip shows the new backdrops; the 月亮線 window strip does not scroll (the car is stopped at the station).

**Why.** Each reading keeps the art baked and the view unchanged where it could, keeps the page data's hit areas, and stays inside the payload and animated-node budgets.

**Consequences.** Later chapters reuse `popFlap`, `visibleWhen` cut-outs, `crossing: 'none'` and `fireflies` as they need them. A landmark vignette on the chapter card, if wanted, is one issue for all chapters.


### D-79 · 2026-10-20 · Chapter 3's pages: coupled parts without cycles, decoys drawn as themselves, the 不要！ hint (#116)

**Context.** #116 writes chapter 3 「醉月湖的鵝」 in full (`docs/design/pages/c3.md`, the D-77 pattern) and builds 3-1 … 3-6 as data. Three things in the outline met the engine and the solver: "bikes push their neighbours" and "moving one shelf shoves its neighbour" with Mocha holding one (3-2, 3-5); a decoy only matters while the bead is hidden (3-3, 3-4, 3-6); and the decoy look was left to this issue by #114/#115.

**Decision.**
1. **Coupled parts are one-way.** A part that is shoved or dragged has no input of its own (`input: 'none'`) and reacts to its neighbour's `partEnter`, guarded by `when: { not: { mochaAt } }` — Mocha sitting on it is the hold (C4's `lockedByCat` only stops the player's hand, and reactions don't read it). 3-2: the outer bikes shove the middle one (the 店員小哥's delivery bike) out across the path; Mocha landing on its seat rolls it back (a perch `set`) and holds it. 3-5: the end shelves have cranks; each inner shelf rolls with its outer neighbour unless Mocha sits on it. Two-way coupling between player-moved parts would be a reaction cycle (lint L12) and makes every unheld first move a soft trap (#183's rule), while in a pure shove system a lock only takes moves away. The 3-2 and 3-5 rows in GDD § 4.4 say so.
2. **Each decoy waits for a lure past a trigger.** 不要！ makes him want the next thing — Mocha — so each decoy page puts the reveal on the way to Mocha: the goose charges when he passes her nest (3-3), the gate's plate is between him and Mocha on its pillar (3-4), and in 3-6 he sits on the very page that turns into Mocha's bridge (`stateWhen: open → not babyAt`), so 不要！ is what frees it. A decoy is never in sight with an unreachable bead (no ties): the bead stays hidden until it is reachable, or the decoy leaves (3-6's glasses are `heldBy` 藍教授 and fly off with her). A check during #116 proved every page's key move necessary.
3. **Decoys are drawn as themselves.** `AttractorDef.art` (view only, GDD § 3.2) names a decoy's look: `decoyArt` draws the moon's reflection on the water, the brass bell, the reading glasses (on her face while she holds them), a silver glint for any other key — never the bead or its cup of light — and his thought bubble shows the decoy, not a bead. #117 replaces the drawings.
4. **Sliders nobody drives are printed art** (`partView`: a tab or slider with `input: 'none'` draws through `StateArtView`), and a tab's or slider's baked bitmap per state is drawn under its knob: a slider's kit art is only its knob, so 3-2's bikes and 3-5's shelves get placeholder drawings (`env/art/c3.ts`, baked).
5. **The stored-solution hint's 不要！ matches on his target.** A `say no` gesture is offered only while he wants what he wanted when the solution said it (`sameRefusal`, `core/hint.ts`): after 3-6's 不要！ the parts are unchanged, and saying it again would refuse Mocha.
6. **Outline changes** (listed in `pages/c3.md`): 3-2's bell is the delivery bike's, rung by tapping Mocha on the seat (the first three pages show at most three touchables); 3-3's 淘氣 is the goose honking at Mocha twice (the chase is the reveal, a tick before the grab); 3-4's Mocha waits on the gate's pillar. Chapter 3's cameo is the 店員小哥 on 3-2 (Mocha's new line 「……連腳踏車也是你的？」), its music box an 8-bar loop in A.

**Why.** It keeps every page solver-green with no dead ends, soft traps, walkable decoys or ties and 0 lint warnings, without new engine features, and keeps the grandparent rule: what is marigold is what you move, and what he wants is drawn as what it is.

### D-80 · 2026-10-20 · Chapter 3's art: the campus kit, a held decoy on its holder's face, the squeeze cut-out (#117)

**Context.** #117 draws 台灣大學 over #116's pages: six backdrops, every part, the goose, 藍教授 and the poster's student, the decoys, the 書, the 土地公's 藍色校工帽 (#116 drew it on the live figure) and 3-5's 淘氣.
