6. **10-2's cameo** fires on his first crawl out at 89F (`babyEnter O1`): the car's arrival and his step are one tick, so `babyAt E` at the car's `partEnter` never held. The 清潔阿伯 carries him over a `walk` edge (the open floor to the window), foreshadowing 10-6.
7. **Placeholders where the preset misleads** (D-95 § 5): a blowable draws a cloud, wrong for 10-1's balloons and 10-4's gold damper sphere — both baked in `env/art/c10.ts`. 10-1's commuter wears 2-1's commuter art (`c10/commuter` in the creature registry); the 土地公's 清潔帽 in `view/art/placeholders.ts`. Everything else keeps its preset's art for #134. An 8-bar music box in C (`chapter10`).

**Why.** Every page is solver-green with no dead ends, soft traps, walkable decoys or ties and 0 lint warnings, and a check proved each key word and perch necessary. Chapter 10 costs ≈ 3.1 s of the solver budget (10-3, 13,340 states, the largest); the suite is ≈ 23 s of 60 for 64 pages. The engine is unchanged; the three shell fixes are game-layer and unit-tested.

**Consequences.** #134 draws the chapter (the tower's sections and crank, the damper and atrium, the elevator, the party, the deck, the 金鎖片) and keeps the hit areas; the sections' ledges currently render as rider tracks over a pit. #135's page E follows 10-6's end. A page that wants Mocha's joke for a word said while linked (none yet) would need a page field.

### D-98 · 2026-10-22 · The solver budget is asserted as work (states), the wall clock reported; chapter 10 made cheaper (#133 fix-forward)

**Context.** #209's deploy failed `pages.test.ts`'s budget: 67.6 s of 60 on the GitHub runner, where this machine measures ≈ 23 s. A first fix (#210) timed the solves with the worker's CPU time instead of the wall clock, on the theory that the parallel test files were the cause; it measured 66.3 s — the runner is simply ≈ 3× slower (≈ 2,100 states a second against the GDD's calibrated 7,000; 8-6 alone, 37,494 states, took 17.9 s there). Even without chapter 10 the runner was at ≈ 59–62 s: the wall-clock budget had been passing by luck.

**Decision.** (1) The budget is asserted as work: the whole suite's explored states under `CI_BUDGET_STATES` = 60 s × 7,000 states/s = **420,000** (`pages/report.ts`; the GDD § 3.7 calibration, D-46). The suite explores 190,291 (45 %). The wall clock is measured as before (`performance.now`) and only warns over 60 s; `npm run solve` still reports it against the 60 s. (2) Chapter 10 made cheaper, with no change to how it plays: 10-3 offers no words (`words: []`, a pure puzzle: 13,340 → 667 states); 10-5's cake lights its candles corner by corner in turn (10,064 → 3,989). Chapter 10 is ≈ 1.5 s here (was 3.1), the suite ≈ 20.3 s.

**Why.** A deploy should fail on content that is too expensive, not on the runner of the day. States are deterministic and are what the GDD's 60 s was derived from (≈ 6,000 states per page on average).

**Consequences.** Page E and the bonus page (#135) have ≈ 230,000 states of headroom. #137's full-game run can revisit the calibration (e.g. against a reference page timed on the machine at hand).

### D-99 · 2026-10-22 · Chapter 10's art: the 101 kit in screen colours, 101 drawn true, a sunrise for the finale, 咻咻's final look (#134)

**Context.** #134 draws chapter 10 (台北101, 05:10) over #133's pages and replaces D-97 § 7's placeholders. Open: the chapter's bake grade is the strongest yet (×1.52, 1.37, 1.19 from midnight), so a kit authored the usual way (dusky, for the bake to lift) would not match the parts, which are never graded; how to draw 101 so it is recognisable at every size; how 10-3's sections, which are rotating `section` discs whose ledges slide sideways, read as the tower; how 10-5's cake reads as a `section` (the view turns sections); how the finale's sunrise looks; what 咻咻 finally looks like; and 10-2's car panel, whose `hit` (a `ridesOn` part's, authored where its node starts) was written at 5F while the car starts at 89F, so the panel drew 224 units below the car.

**Decision.**
1. **The palette kit** `POPUP_101` (bible § 6 row 10): dawn violet and teal glass dominant, peach and gold the accents, every colour ≥ ΔE 12 from marigold (`affordance.test.ts`). It is in **screen colours**: every chapter 10 backdrop is passed through `ungradeSvg` (`env/grade.ts`: the chapter's grade divided out, keeping what is never graded), so the bake's grade lands it back on the kit (within 1 ΔE, tested) and the parts, live cast and ledges share one kit with the backdrops.
2. **101 drawn true** (`tower101`, `section101`): the podium (a truncated pyramid with the gold coin), eight flared sections of eight floors each with three facets (left indigo, front teal glass with its sheen, right peach), floor mullions, a cyan rim on each lip, gold 如意 on the lowest, the stepped crown, the silver spire and its red light (proportions 0.2 / 0.53 / 0.13 / 0.14). It stands ahead in 10-1's front window, over 10-3's terrace (the sections are the puzzle; the upper five recede looking up), as the crown and spire behind 10-5 and 10-6, and as souvenirs in 10-2's gift shop. 信義's towers (`xinyiSkyline`), 象山 and the 四獸山 (`eastHills`), vellum cloud strips lit from below (`dawnClouds`, gold on 10-6) and the cloud wrapping the tower (`cloudWrap`).
3. **10-3.** The ledges are carriages riding the sections (`sectionLedge`, `view/art/taipei101.ts`, under both section art keys): balcony slabs of the lip with a gold-railed glass balustrade, so the sections' sideways turns read; `trackPit: false`, no rails. The bottom and top sections are grey-lilac medallions (bible § 6: fixed sections are grey-lilac; only Mocha holds them) showing the section from above, its three facets as sectors, teal to the front when in line; the crank is a marigold knurled wheel on the window-washer's drive.
4. **10-5.** The table is the backdrop's; the cake (a `section`, turned a fifth a state by the view) is drawn from above on a turntable, a candle lit a state. The back of the ring round the table is a thin step (`roofStep`) so the table shows.
5. **10-6, the sunrise:** the sky walks to gold behind 象山, the sun a foil disc clearing the ridge with its halo and vellum rays, origami birds flying into it, the crown's sun-facing edges gold-rimmed, the sun's path across the deck to where he takes his steps (the photo's frame). The rail at his chest ends in a capped post at x 152 (R2); past it, only the deck and sky.
6. **咻咻's final look** (cast sheet): a pillow-sized puff with big glossy blue anime eyes, a blush, a typhoon swirl and a wisp of wind; **crying** in its rain cloud (rain grey, tears streaming), **lonely** on the floor (pale blue-grey, a tear), **calm** once hugged (`calm`, `up`: white, happy eyes, sparkles). Live, flat fills. It changes 咻咻 everywhere it appears (9-5 and the linked toy pages too).
7. **10-2's panel hit** is moved to where the car starts (89F: `{ x: 208, y: 170 }`), as a riding part's hit is authored (1-5's); where it stands at 5F is unchanged. The issue's "keep the hit areas" means the hit areas players touch: this one was drawn and hit-tested 224 units off the car.
8. The cast (`taipei101.ts`): the 清潔阿伯 (pale-blue uniform, the 清潔帽, mop and bucket; baked per look), the sleeping 上班族 (`aisle`, `seat`). The 金鎖片 is drawn (`treasureArt`: a 如意-shaped gold lock on its red cord, 「平安」, three bells). New ledge styles: `observatoryFloor`, `ticketHall`, `plaza101`, `atriumBalcony`, `damperCase`, `roofTerrace`, `roofStep`, `skyDeck`, `railFooting`.
9. The photo frames, the chapter-card vignette, the 目錄 station illustration and the 月亮線 window strip stay with #193 (as D-96 § 8).

**Why.** One kit across baked and live art removes a whole class of mismatches the dusky-authoring convention needed iteration to hide (D-96: "the first car came out washed-out pink"). Every page stays under 350 KB and keeps #133's hit areas (but the panel's, § 7) and node positions; no engine or sim change.

**Consequences.** Later chapters (page E at 10:00 has an identity grade) can author in screen colours the same way. #135's page E and the bonus page can reuse `tower101`, `eastHills`, `sunrise`.

### D-100 · 2026-10-22 · The ending: Mocha as page E's goal, 尾聲 as chapter 11, the 05:30 scene, credits as cards over the kept prints (#135)

**Context.** #135 builds the ending (GDD § 4.4 page E, § 5.5, § 2.13 item 8). Open: how "a `mocha` attractor marked `goal`" works when Mocha moves between perches and lint L11 rejects a non-shiny goal; what happens when he hops (no Mocha candidate while travelling — Baokaka would grab a treasure, breaking the one moment the rule breaks); whether page E offers 一起 (#128: linked, he never grabs a Mocha goal); where page E sits in the registry, the 目錄 and the album; how the 05:30 scene, the credits and the post-credits play; the order of the photo, the first words and the album; the credits' content (not in § 5).

**Decision.**
1. **`PageDef.mochaGoal`** (page E only): Mocha's own candidate (kind `mocha`, id `'mocha'`) is the goal; arriving at him Baokaka cuddles him, emits `grab` of `'mocha'` and the page is done. While Mocha travels he stays a candidate at the perch he is heading for (`reachNode`), so with `priorityOverride: { mocha: 0 }` nothing else is ever the target. Lint rule 11 on a `mochaGoal` page: no goal attractor, Mocha starts on the page, no box perch, 一起 not sayable. `photoReady` and the album treat Mocha as the treasure (`treasureOf` → `'mocha'`, always +M).
2. **No words on page E** (`words: []`): 一起 would link them (#128); 不要！ to Mocha would make him grab a treasure.
3. **尾聲 is chapter 11** (`c11p1`, `pages/end/e.ts`, `ENDING_CHAPTER`), **番外 chapter 12** (`BONUS_CHAPTER`, PR 2): `EXTRA_CHAPTERS` names them (尾聲 「抓周」 家 10:00; 番外 「象山的日出」), outside `CHAPTERS` (the map's eleven stations). The 目錄 lists page E on 家's strip (marked 尾聲; `stationOf`). Page E is a spread; the 10:00 HUD chip shows the sun.
4. **Held lines.** The family's cheers are page E's intro, held (`lines.introPause`, new); 「……經過了書／算盤／雞腿？」 are held `babyEnter` lines on T1–T3 (not on the node where Mocha is). The 05:30 lines are their own scene; 「早上十點，抓周開始了！」 is the card's line.
5. **Order after the grab:** the photo moment frames the grab (as on every spread); then **his first words first** (the end lines, six beats), then the **抓周 photo** opens in the album (so 爸爸's 「……第三個字一定是爸爸。」 comes after the first two), then the sticker card, the credits, the post-credits, the cover. The 抓周 photo is the album's last leaf (`ALBUM_LEAVES` 12), chapter 11, § 5.6 row E for every frame, 「第一個字」 摩卡 printed big instead of a word learned (`firstWord`).
6. **The 05:30 scene and the post-credits** are `StoryScene`s: a baked picture (`dawn-scene`, `shrine-scene`, screen art in `env/art/end.ts`) laid on the quilt, its lines a tap a beat. Before page E: after 10-6's sticker card, then page E opens with its card.
7. **The credits** are cards, not a scroll (so reduced motion only fades): the title, then each kept chapter print in order drifting onto the endpapers with the next credit, the leftover credits alone, then 「寶咖咖，一歲生日快樂。」 over the 抓周 photo. 4.2 s a card or a tap; 「跳過」 ends them. The credits (`content/credits.ts`, not in § 5): the cast as the night met them — 寶咖咖, 摩卡 (哥哥，兼翻譯), 咻咻, the family, 廣播姊姊, 店員小哥, 背包客 (his 「I found it!」 cameo at 101's foot), 藍教授 and 黑糖, 「戴不同帽子的阿伯」 (the post-credits reveal) — and 製作 「寶咖咖的爸爸・Claude」 (the owner may change it). UI strings not in § 5.7: 跳過, 第一個字 and the scenes' accessible names.
8. **Art.** `POPUP_HOME_DAY` (the home in daylight, screen colours through `ungradeSvg`, 10:00's grade being ×1.8) and `POPUP_ENDING_SCENES`; the family are the cast's die-cut grown-ups (`adult`), generic and warm (§ 6 risk 11); the play-mat in the sun (`playmatDay`); the treasures are drawn on the tray in the backdrop (`art: 'baked'` attractors draw nothing live). 尾聲's standee grade is identity (no sprite variant). The music box: chapter 0's (home again).
9. Small fixes: the hint fallback answers a wait on an `allowTrivial` page with no gestures (it restarted forever); a par of 0 is in band for an `allowTrivial` page; `check:play`'s `readTalk` reads on a held line that follows the one just read a tick later.

**Why.** Mocha as the goal by a page flag keeps the attractor model (static nodes) intact and the rule exact; staying wanted while he hops keeps the story true in every reachable state (the solver proves 0 dead ends, no treasure consumed in the unit test). Cards for the credits meet the reduced-motion rule (opacity only) and the 48 px / 20 px rules with no scrolling text.

**Consequences.** Page E costs 19 solver states. #136 audits the new screens; #137 sees page E's par 0 (allowed). PR 2 adds the bonus page (chapter 12) and its unlock.

### D-101 · 2026-10-22 · The bonus page 「象山的日出」: a post-game page outside the order, a leaf 咻咻 blows (#135, part 2)

**Context.** GDD § 2.8 and § 4.4 give the bonus page, which all 65 土地公 unlock, as a gentle sunrise on the 象山 trail with 咻咻 as a breeze to ride ("blowable leaves"). The 土地公 gives the 「台北走透透」 stamp. #93 asked that the bonus page stay out of the linear `furthest` order, so that finishing page E never unlocks it. #113's 目錄 postcard showed a 未完待續 panel until now.

**Decision.**
1. **Post-game pages in the registry** (`pages/index.ts` `BONUS`, `registry.bonus`, `ALL_PAGES`):
   - `pageById` and `chapterOf` know them.
   - `pages`, `nextPage`, `furthest` and the § 2.8 reward counts don't.
   - The bonus page sets its own `words` (`['whoosh']`) and growth.
   - The solver proof, the hint test, the bake, the art report and the lab cover `ALL_PAGES`. The sprite sync doesn't: 番外's standee grade is identity at 05:30.
2. **Progress** (`progress.ts`):
   - `isBonusUnlocked(save)` is true when every page of the 65-page book has its 土地公. It is read off the save, never stored.
   - `isUnlocked` and `earnSticker` follow it.
   - `completePage` of the bonus page records the solve and stickers but moves neither `furthest` nor `current`, and `openPage` never makes it the page to resume.
   - `decodeSave` keeps its record.
3. **The page** (`c12p1`, 番外, chapter 12, 05:30):
   - Baokaka sits on a huge fallen leaf at the gully's edge. The leaf is a `blowable` (`near` · `mid` · `far`). 咻—— (咻咻's breeze, drawn on the leaf part) blows it a stop at a time across the gully, then he climbs the 六巨石 boulder to the bead.
   - Par 2, 24 states, no traps.
   - 淘氣: Mocha rides the leaf with him.
   - 土地公: on the boulders in a 登山帽.
   - When the bead is his, the end lines are the 土地公's stamp: 「呵呵，囡仔，慢慢仔大。」 and 「土地公蓋了最後一個章：「台北走透透」。」 (`x.stamp.2`, not in § 5: a narration line of my own). Then the sticker card, then the 目錄 at the postcard.
4. **The 目錄's postcard panel** holds the bonus page's card (番外, 象山 · 05:30) and its 淘氣. Tapping the card plays the page, with 番外's chapter card first. The 未完待續 line stays only as the fallback for a registry without the page.
5. **Art** (`env/art/bonus.ts`, `POPUP_XIANGSHAN`, in screen colours through `ungradeSvg(…, 12)`): the sun clearing the hills behind 101 (chapter 10's `tower101`, `sunrise`, `eastHills`, `xinyiSkyline`, now exported), the trail's trees, stone steps and rail, the gully of ferns, the boulders and the trail sign. The colour clock gains 番外's dawn (chapter 12, 05:30), outside the night's time order.

**Why.** Holding the bonus page outside the order keeps every existing rule (linear unlocks, the rewards' counts, resume) untouched while letting the shell, the solver and the art pipeline treat it like any page.

**Consequences.** A debug save opens it: the fixture `contents-bonus-open` (every 土地公 found) and `check:play`'s `bonus-open` scenario, which plays from the postcard to the solve and back to the 目錄.

### D-102 · 2026-10-22 · The photo frames drawn per chapter, and 哇！'s own pose (#193, part 1)

**Context.** Since #103 (D-66), every album photo has been a composite: the gameplay poses `bounce`, `reach` and `eat` with the chapter's flat `treasureArt` laid over them, so the 雞腿 floated above his hands and every chapter's photo had the same body. #193 asks for the character artist's real frames A, B, C (+M) per chapter, with the treasure in his hands or mouth, through the art v2 rig. 哇！ was said with the shared `point` frame. Open: where the frames live in the bake (they are not gameplay poses), at what scale, how a frame is composed (the treasure is in the frame, not over it), what +M is, and which chapters get frames.

**Decision.**
1. **One pose per chapter, three frames.** `baokaka.photo<N>` (N = 1 … 10): frame 0 is A, 1 is B, 2 is C. The rig (`docs/art/v2/src/ph_poses.py`) draws the ten treasures as character-art props (tapered ink, cel shading, gold foil), and the frames hold them: A lifts it high beside his face with both hands, his face the 「哇！」 face (the new `wow` expression), two glints off it; B crawls in, his near hand out to it on the floor ahead; C sits chewing it, eyes shut in glee (`chomp`), its bitten end at his mouth (the mouth point is computed from the head rig). A wide treasure (書, 算盤) is held to his mouth by one hand. Chapter 10 is the photo of the first steps: B walks in, A stands (the `wobble` stance) holding the 金鎖片 up, C has plopped down to chew it.
2. **Photo space, photo scale.** Each frame is composed in the photo's own space: the anchor is the photo's floor centre, which `PhotoScene` puts at the crop's (150, 200); its box (`POSE_VB`) is wide enough for B's crawl and the treasure ahead. It is baked at the photo's print scale (`POSE_PT` ×1.45, the composite's draw scale), so the viewfinder draws it 1 : 1. `bake_poses.py` reads both per pose; the hashes of the other poses are unchanged, so nothing else re-rendered.
3. **+M** is `mocha.photo`, also at ×1.45, one frame per photo frame: A 「哇！」 with him (sat up, head turned up to the treasure, eyes round), B the album figure facing the camera, C the glare (「那是阿嬤的，不能吃。」). He stays in the bottom-left corner.
4. **The composite stays as the fallback** for a chapter without frames (`photoFrames(chapter)` null: the lab stage, a future chapter). The 抓周 photo (chapter 11) keeps its pair from #135: Mocha is the treasure there, and its frames (hug + glare) were already chosen for it.
5. **Loading.** The frames are group `'ui'`, never mirrored, and a photo preloads only its own chapter's frames (`photoSprites([chapter])`); the credits preload the chapters they show. They cost ≈ 57–65 KB a chapter @3x and are outside every page's preload.
6. **哇！** gets `baokaka.wow` (`WORD_POSE.wow`): sat, both arms flung up in a wide V, leaning back, eyes wide, mouth a big round O (the `wow` mouth). Preload group 4 (learnt on 4-3), so the graded variants exist for chapters 4–10. The sprite report lists it and the photo frames.
7. **The bake's top tolerance.** The standee bake's core trims hairline strands (his cowlick's tips); at ×1.45 these stand up to 1.5 pt above the rest, so the manifest test allows that for the photo frames only.

**Why.** Drawing the treasure into the frame is the only way his hands hold it (the composite could only lay it over a pose). Composing in photo space keeps `PhotoScene` trivial (one sprite at one point) and lets the artist compose each chapter's photo, including B's distance to the treasure. Baking at the print scale keeps the hero moment crisp.

**Consequences.** The live B no longer slides in (`approach` only moves the composite): the frame is a composed photograph and the viewfinder's creep still moves. A new chapter adds its treasure to `ph_poses.TREASURES`, a `HOLD` and a `SPEC` row, then `bake_poses.py` and `npm run sync:sprites`. #193's next parts draw the chapter-card vignettes, the 目錄 station illustrations and the 月亮線 window strip.

### D-103 · 2026-10-22 · The landmark vignettes: one drawing per chapter, a die-cut behind the chapter card and a medallion for the 目錄's station (#193, part 2)

**Context.** D-78 § 9 and D-80 § 6 left a landmark vignette on the chapter card "if wanted". #122 added a 目錄 station illustration per chapter (the map drew every station as the same disc). The card's text sizes and contrast must not change (R5, R6), and no lettering may go into the art.

**Decision.**
1. **One drawing per chapter** (`env/art/vignettes.ts`): the prologue's 家 (two old 公寓, his window lit), 台北車站's 重簷 hall, the 大安 amphitheatre shell, 傅鐘 between the palms, 紅樓 with a neon sign, 士林's awnings under a bulb string, 松山's tower and an origami jet, a 貓空 gondola over the tea terraces, 北投's green-roofed library with its steam, 淡江大橋, and 101 over the 信義 skyline. Each is drawn in a 150 × 70 space in its chapter kit's colours and graded like the chapter's backdrops (chapter 10's kit is already in screen colours). Baked as screen art twice (`npm run bake:popup`).
2. **On the card** (`vignette-card-<N>`): the landmark as a die-cut pop-up (the sprite bake's standee filter: warm-white border, cut line, shadow). It stands *behind* the card and rises over the card's top edge between the strings, left of where the time chip lifts. Nothing on the card moves, so its words keep their sizes and contrast. What floats (cables, steam, mist, bulbs on a wire, fireflies) is drawn over the die-cut, never inside its border. 尾聲's card shows 家 again; 番外 has none (its postcard is its picture).
3. **On the 目錄** (`vignette-map-<N>`): an open station is the landmark in a round medallion against the chapter's night sky (stars, the moon), with a ring (foil when the chapter is complete, cream with the ribbon's red edge when it is open) and the chapter's number on a foil tag. A locked station stays the padlocked dimple, so it gives nothing away. The medallions load with the map (`useScreenReady`).
4. **Cost.** 11 cut-outs at 3.7–6.8 KB and 11 medallions at 1.5–2.4 KB (@2x). The first load is still ≈ 1.4 MB of 2.5 MB.
5. **Fixtures.** `chapter-card-c2` … `chapter-card-c9` join `-p`, `-c1` and `-c10`. While the card's vignette loads, its image is marked `data-sprites="loading"`, so stills wait for it.

**Why.** A drawing behind the card is the only place that changes nothing on the card. Sharing one drawing between the card and the map gives each chapter one recognisable picture, and keeps the cost small.

**Consequences.** A new chapter adds a `LANDMARKS` row. Part 3 draws the 月亮線 window strip.

### D-104 · 2026-10-22 · The 月亮線 window strip: a baked loop behind a matte-cut frame, only on the cars that are moving (#193, part 3)

**Context.** The bible (§ 6, the cars row) says a page-1 car's window shows the chapter's skyline sliding by as a looping strip, "the only scrolling backdrop". Until now the window was part of the baked backdrop and stood still (D-78 § 9). The strip has to be:
- a baked looping skyline moved by a CSS transform loop;
- still in screenshots and under reduced motion / 減少動態;
- counted in the animated budget;
- one transformed group per car.

Open: how a live layer can pass *behind* what hangs in front of a window (straps, poles, frames, the glass's sheen) without redrawing each backdrop's foreground.

**Decision.**
1. **Two baked cut-outs per moving car** (`env/art/strips.ts`, `env/windowStrips.ts`). Both are graded with the chapter. Chapter 10's strip, like its backdrops, is drawn in screen colours, so its grade is undone first.
   - **The strip** (`<backdrop>-strip`): one period of the view. The tile is drawn at −P, 0 and +P and clipped to [0, P], so whatever overhangs one edge comes back in at the other, and the loop is seamless by construction. Ridges are sums of whole-period waves.
   - **The frame** (`<backdrop>-frame`): the backdrop over the window band, with the windows cut out.
2. **The frame is cut by difference matting.** Each car's window view goes through `windowView()`. While the frame bakes, `WINDOW_MATTE` replaces the view with a flat white matte, then a flat black one (`rgb()`, so the grade leaves it alone). `bake-popup.ts` computes alpha = 1 − (white − black) and colour = black / alpha. Straps, poles and frames keep their exact coverage, and the sheen stays as a soft overlay, with no per-backdrop layer surgery. The backdrop itself is unchanged: the turn's leaves and the 目錄's page cards still show its still view.
3. **The live layer** `strip` (new, between `backdrop` and `cutouts`; animated; not a fold group, since it lies flat on the page): `WindowStrip` repeats the strip across the band inside one `data-anim` group, which moves left one period on a linear CSS loop (`--ws-period`). The frame image lies over it. It holds still under reduced motion (the OS's or 減少動態), in low-power mode and in stills (`[data-still]`). The strip's clip is inset 2 units from the band, so no sliver shows along the frame bitmap's soft edge.
4. **Which cars:** the moving ones.

   | Page | View in the strip | Period |
   |---|---|---|
   | 1-1 | the night city (a tiny 101 once a period) | 9 s |
   | 5-1 | the tunnel's lamps streaking past | 3.2 s |
   | 6-1 | the side window: the city from the 文湖線's viaduct and the apron's lights; the big front window stays as it was | 11 s |
   | 8-1 | the 北投 hills, steam and park lamps | 14 s |
   | 9-1 | 觀音山 across the river at dawn | 18 s |
   | 10-1 | 信義 and the hills going peach | 14 s |

   **2-1, 3-1 and 4-1 keep their still windows.** Their cars stand at a platform with the doors opening (their windows show the station's far wall), and a sliding view would say the train is moving. 7-1 is a station, not a car. This is a reading of the issue's "every page-1 car": the strip goes on every car that is travelling.
5. **Cost.** Strip and frame together: 1-1 66 KB, 5-1 61 KB, 6-1 33 KB, 8-1 110 KB, 9-1 78 KB, 10-1 67 KB. They are counted in page art (`pageArt`) and preloaded with the page (`pageArtUrls`). Every page stays ≤ 350 KB (5-1 is the highest at 326 KB). Animated nodes: one group and two or three images per page.

**Why.** Matting the frame from two renders works on any backdrop, whatever crosses its windows. The strip is two small bitmaps and one transform, which is cheap at 4× CPU throttle (≈ 55–60 fps on 1-1, 5-1 and 8-1).

**Consequences.** A new moving car adds a `WINDOW_STRIPS` row, a tile in `TILES`, and `windowView()` round its view.

### D-105 · 2026-10-22 · Self-hosted font subsets cut to the game's text, sliced by play order (#68; supersedes D-17's Google Fonts)

**Context.** The Google Fonts link (D-17, D-27) cost a 141 KB render-blocking stylesheet and ≈ 1.6 MB of unicode-range slices for the cover and page 0-1 (measured by `npm run check:prod`: 330 KB on the cover, 1447 KB more after 「開始」). Google's slices group glyphs by general frequency, so a short CJK screen touches many of them. The issue asks for ≤ 60 KB render-blocking and ≤ 250 KB of fonts for the cover and the first page, with no glyph falling back to the system font.

**Decision.**
1. **Self-host** Chiron GoRound TC 700/900, Noto Serif TC 700/900 and Fredoka 500/600/700, the faces and weights the old link loaded. The look does not change. All three are SIL OFL 1.1 without a Reserved Font Name, so subsetting and self-hosting are allowed; the licenses ship in `public/fonts/`, and each file keeps its copyright and license name records.
2. **Sources.** The variable fonts in google/fonts at a pinned commit, checked by SHA-256. Each weight is instanced the way Google builds its static files (`wght`; `wdth` 100 for Fredoka). The subsets keep the layout features a browser applies on its own, plus `tnum` (`tabular-nums`) and `halt`/`chws`/`vhal`/`vchw` (Chrome's `text-spacing-trim`). Stylistic sets and alternates are dropped: with them, the first file was 72 KB instead of 37 KB.
3. **The character set** comes from the source, not the build, so the test runs before `vite build`. It counts string literals, templates and JSX text in the shipped modules plus printable ASCII, and skips comments (`src/fonts/charset.ts`): 1,049 characters. The dev tools (the art lab with the RPG, PageLab) add 316 more, in a `dev.css` that only they import.
4. **Slices in play order.** File 0 holds 239 characters, ≈ 35–40 KB a face:
   - printable ASCII;
   - every punctuation mark and symbol. Chrome trims the space between CJK punctuation (`text-spacing-trim`) only within one font file, so 『一起』。 set wide when 』 and 。 were in different slices;
   - what a first session actually shows, recorded in a browser (`npm run fonts:session`: cover → 開始 → page 0-1 with its lines, 114 characters).

   The other characters follow by the chapter that first prints them (script keys, page folders, chapter tables), then by use, 110 to a file. Each `@font-face` lists its file's exact `unicode-range`. The chapter guess only decides *which* file a glyph is in, never *whether* it is covered.
5. **The guard is a test, not CI tooling.** CI has no Python, so the generated files are committed. `fonts.test.ts` parses each WOFF2's cmap in JS (brotli from `node:zlib`). It fails when the source prints a character some CJK weight has no file for, and when a range promises a glyph its file lacks. Only ✗ ❗ ❚ ⟲, which neither font ever drew, fall back to the system font, as before.
6. **`check:prod` gates the payload.** It fails over 60 KB of external font stylesheets or 250 KB of font files for the cover and page 0-1, and lists each request.

**Result** (`npm run check:prod`, throttled 4G, cold cache). These are single runs on a dev container, so the timings are ±50 ms.

| | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| Render-blocking font CSS | 141 KB (Google `css2`) | 0 KB external. The `@font-face` rules add ≈ 4.5 KB gzip to the app stylesheet. |
| Font files: cover | 189 KB, 3 files | 70 KB, 2 files |
| Font files: 開始 → page 0-1 | 1447 KB, 15 files | 80 KB, 3 files |
| First load to the cover | 818 KB | 589 KB |
| 開始 → page 0-1 | +1676 KB | +634 KB |

The whole game's fonts are 1.0 MB over 39 files, fetched a slice at a time as chapters bring new characters. The repo holds 1.5 MB of WOFF2, dev slices included.

**Why.** Text subsetting through Google's `text=` parameter would need one URL per screen and keeps the render-blocking stylesheet. Dropping a weight changes the look. A subset of the game's own text is the smallest option, and it removes the site's only external dependency.

**Consequences.**
- New text with a new character fails `npm run test` until `npm run fonts` is run and committed. This needs Python with fonttools and brotli.
- Re-run `npm run fonts:session` when the cover or page 0-1 change their text.
- The art bakes (`bake:popup`, `docs/art/popup/src/render.mjs`) keep loading Google Fonts. They are offline tools, and their lettering ends up as bitmaps.
